Catalog number : M00317
Uniform title: vārāhītantra
Manuscript : NGMCP manuscript no.: 3-315 Reel No. : B 144/9
Description:
Description by Mark S.G. Dyczkowski:
There is another texts with this name which is much shorter and is
framed as a dialogue between Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. This Vārāhī Tantra is
4,631 verses long. Another complete manuscript is deposited in
the library of Saṃpūrṇānanda Sanskrit University in Vārāṇasi.

Vārāhī, is one of the eight Mothers (mātṛkā). Although
Tantras bearing the names of the other Mothers have not been
recovered, Lakṣmīdhara quotes a verse in his commentary on the
Saundaryalaharī which lists them as a standard group of Tantras.
This Vārāhītantra associates itself with Kāmarūpa which is where it
may possibly have been redacted. The colophons state that it belongs
to the Dakṣiṇāmnāya. It draws extensively from other Tantras but
also contains much original material. Indeed, it is a veritable
compendium of śākta ritual procedures. It is quoted in several
texts. These include the Mantramahārṇava,
Tārābhaktisudhārṇava, āgamakalpalatā,
Saubhāgyabhāskara, Tārārahasyavṛtti,
āgamatattvavilāsa, Sarvollāsatantra, the Bengali Tantrasāra
and Lakṣmīdharas commentary on the Saundaryalaharī. It is also
quoted in the Puraścaryārṇava which is attributed to the Nepalese
king, Pratāpasiṃhaśāha. It is known to have been which was
completed in 1775 ce, thus establishing the terminus ante quem
for the date of the Vārāhītantra. Incidently, this fact
demonstrates that the aforementioned texts must all be later.


Practically all colophons are in the following simple format:

iti śrīvārāhītaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade
mahātirahasyataṃtrāvatāre prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 1 |
Notes:
Revision 0: Nov. 15, 2014

Data-entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the direction of Mark S.G. Dyczkowski.
Publication country : India
########################################################################### 



p. 1a)

oṃ namaḥ śrīsuṃdarīkālikāvārāhīdevyai namaḥ ||

kāmarūpe mahāpīṭhe ruṇḍamuṇḍoparisthite |
nānāgaṇasamākīrṇe vicitrayoginīganā ||

yasyā yogya samādāya vāhanaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak |
tanmadhya saṃsthitā vīraḥ pracaṇḍa sūkarāsanaḥ ||

bhāvayukta namaskṛtya prapraccha guhyakālikā |
yuktvāmṛta kathaṃ nātha sarvatra śreṣṭha kiṃ bhavet ||

kautukaṃ ca mayāścaryamapavitrapavitrakaṃ |
vārāhīsaṃjñayā taṃtraṃ dhyānāgamakathaṃ prabho ||

kiṃcit smaraṇamātreṇa caturvarga prakīrtitā |
namaskṛtya punaprāha vismayotphullalocanaṃ ||

śrīcaṇḍabhairava uvāca ||

śṛṇu kāli pravakṣyāmi cittamādāyagoptitaṃ |
purākṛtayugasyārdhe vikhyātā bhuvanatrayaṃ ||

viḍāla rākṣasaḥ proktaṃ gajaśūlāyudhākaraṃ |
upadrutā jagat sarve yuddhājaya mahāvalaṃ ||

tasyā upāsitā devī vārāhī sumukhī satī |
tasyā vaśya yadā dāsī ājñā kālī ca tatparaḥ ||

kadācit nāmamātreṇa vektitā devatāśraya |
kampitāṃgotibhītiśca nisvāsalupta saṃsthite ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

kutrasthā gṛhasaṃsthāne digdvāramārgapālakāḥ |
evaṃ vīrakathaṃ hatvā marditā kena bhairava ||

satyaṃ vrūhi mahāvāho koladhvaja mahāvalaḥ |
kāṃ vidyāṃ lahatā devi viḍālasya vadhārthakam ||

p. 1b)

pracaṃḍauvāca ||

śṛṇu devī varārohe hetuyukta vadāmyahaṃ |
vanavāsagate pūrva rāmalakṣmaṇajānakī ||

rāvaṇasya vadhārthāya devatā nara saṃbhavaḥ |
kiṃcit kālāṃtare tatra vanaikāṃtara jānakī ||

dānave haritā śītāddhṛte laṃkāpurī gatā |
vedehīṃ ca na dṛṣṭāyaṃ rāmalakṣmaṇaroditaṃ ||

bhramatāvana sarvasthā vana śailaṃ ca jaṃgamaḥ |
teṣāṃ labhya mahādevī daivasaṃyogasaṃyutaṃ ||

śaila ṛkṣa mukhasthāne kapiṃdragaṇasanmukhe |
labhaṃte rāmacaṃdrāgre vismṛtaṃ kapi pṛcchataṃ ||

kastvaṃ manuṣyarodāyaṃ kiṃ duḥkhaṃ ca samāgamaḥ |
vaktrā śighraṃ tu tatsarvaṃ vastracihnaṃ dadāti ca ||

pracaṇḍauvāca ||

ahaṃ na bhāṣitā devī rāmāyaṇa vicitratā |
muktamāṇikya sauvarṇaṃ graṃthitālacitā yathā ||

vaśiṣṭha uvāca ||

vaśiṣṭhalacitā graṃtha ko vruvaṃti pṛthak pṛthak |
rāmavāṇa prayogena vālī prāṇa hatā gatā ||

mitrasaṃbhāṣanaśceti rāmasugrīvamarkaṭaḥ |
parasparaupakāreṇa prītimanyonyavarddhanaṃ ||

teṣāṃ sugrīvamutthāya nāmāgre ca nivedayet |
śṛṇu vāli sutādīni sahaṃtīsoganādhipa ||

sarvasthāne caturdikṣu kutrasthā jānakīsajī |
dṛṣṭvā saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śighraṃ dṛṣṭāmihā gatā ||

iti śrutvā vālimukha valavīrya parākrama |
rāmacaṃdrādi sarveṣāṃ yogyamārga namaskṛtā ||

rāmalaṃkṛtamādāya uditā tikṣṇanāditā |
laṃkāṃ gatvā caturdikṣu dṛśyatā kapi vallabhaḥ ||

p. 2a)

teṣāṃmaśoka vanikā vaidehī dṛśyatā kapi |
namaskṛtvāti guptena yathā yuktena laṃkṛta ||

pūrṇakuṃbhe pataṃtī ca mayinī madhyaguṃthita |
yuktasaṃbhāṣaṇaścaiva jānakī laṃkṛtālayau ||

namaskṛtvā praṇāmaṃ ca punaudditā mahākapiḥ |
prāptiṃtā śighrarāmāgre namaskṛtya praṇamyati ||

vākyā laṃkṛtadāsyāya rāmacaṃdrāgra vistarāt |
evaṃ śrutvā rāghavānāṃ sugrīvādi gaṇai saha ||

caturaṃgāneka sainya valavīrya pratāpavān |
sarvaṃ gatvā ca saṃgrāma dvāralaṃkā tadāśraya ||

labhaṃte sahasainyeṣāṃ ghoravigrahanāditā |
tat śabda śruti lābhāya sa sainya saṃyutā gataḥ ||

viḍāla tikṣṇa daṃṣṭrā ca gaja śūtvā yudha dvayaḥ |
aneka gaṇa saṃkīrṇa yoddhutā sanmukha svayaṃ ||

teṣāṃ krodha samādāya hanumaṃtodditāya vai |
lohamudgaramādāya hanninā patitā yathā ||

kāṣṭhapāṣāṇaśailasthā yathā patitarākṣasa |
iti dṛṣṭā viḍāloyaṃ smared bhīti parāyate ||

vārāhī mātamātaṃhīsaṃkastohaṃtareti ca ||

viḍālauvāca ||

āgatosi mahādevī śūlakharparadhārinī |
saṃkaṣṭatāriṇī devī vārāhī bhayahāriṇī ||

iti śrutvā jagad vyāpī mātā tribhuvaneśvarī |
kārtiśūladharāyaṃ vai dvārāgre śighra labhyate ||

hūṁ hūṁ hūṁkāra nādāyaṃ ghasmaraṃti sa sainya ca |
grāsitā ghaṭitā sarve hanumat rāma varjayet ||

p. 2b)

idaṃ dṛṣṭyā rāmacaṃdra murcchitoyaṃ nipātitā |
dṛśyatā vali mukhyābhi vismṛtoyaṃ mahāvala ||

roditārādhanaṃ kṛtvā durvāsā muni mauktikā |
nāthanāthādi śīṣṭātvaṃ satvarāgamatatparaṃ ||

maṃtravāṇasaraścāpakoṭyāgni teja mālaya |
śrīrāmacaṃdrarakṣārthaṃ āgatosi jagadguru ||

dhyānārthī muniśārdūla saṃketa śabdaśrūyate |
vāyuvega mahāmaṃtra smared gamana satvaraṃ ||

dhāvaṃti laṃkāgre tatra laṃbhate kapi sanmukha |
kapiṃ dṛṣṭvā dvijaṃ dāchasa sainya kutragāminā ||

markaṭauvāca ||

śṛṇu vipra mahānāthamidaṃ bhagavati svayaṃ |
ghasmared grāsitā sarve sugrīvalakṣmaṇādina ||

mayābhaubhītitā sthitvā tasyā smaraṇa satvara |
kiṃ karomi mahāvipra yukta prāpti sa sainyakā ||

durvāsauvāca ||

sāmṛtodakasiṃcoyaṃ veṭitārāghavaṃ dṛśet |
muni pāda samādāya praṇamparoditaṃ sthitaṃ ||

dṛṣṭoti munikāruṇyaṃ dvikare vāhu pṛṣṭhatā |
utthāya cuṃvitā vaktrā na rudaṃti mahānṛpa ||

vāmaurau rāmasaṃsthāpya dakṣarau sthāpyate kapi |
jāpitā muni vipreṃdra vārāhi maṃtranāyikā ||

maṃtravāṇa dhanuścaṃdra vāṇamaṃtra su siddhidā |
tasyāsau tyaktatāṃ gatvā devyāgre muni satvaraṃ ||

stotraṃ kṛtvā muniṃdroyaṃ maṃtraṃ ca japamācaret |
asya śrīmaṃtravārāhyā durvāsā ṛṣayastathā ||

chaṃdonuṣṭup mahādevī vārāhiṃ devatā smṛtāḥ |
sarvaduṣṭāribhaṃgā ca viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||

p. 3a)

vāgbhavaṃ pūrvamuccārya māyāvījaṃ tato vadet |
lakṣmīvījaṃ tataḥ paścā khphreṁ vījaṃ samuddharet ||

vārāhī ca tato ṅeṃtāṃ vahnijāyāṃtako manuḥ ||

cauhārapīṭhanilayāṃ vara śūkarāsyāṃ ghāṇāvihāraṇa parāṃ 
mahiṣāsanasthāṃ |
hūṃkāra pīṭhanilayāṃgaṇaveṣṭaṃ mānāṃ vārāhikāṃ bhavaṃbhayakaraṇī 
namāmi || 

caṇḍāsurāriharaṇī mṛgarājasaṃsthāśaṃkheṃdukuṃdadhavalāṃ 
kamalāyatākṣī |
pāśāṃkuśākṣadadhatī munidevavaṃdyāṃ pāyāt sadāvarunadigdala 
śobhitāsāṃ ||

oṃ hrīṁ glūṁ ḷṁ vrīṁ svāhā ||

iti te kathitaṃ kāli vārāhi maṃtramuttamaṃ |
utpatti kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kiṃ manyacchrotumicchasi ||

iti śrīvārāhītaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasyataṃtrāvatāre 
prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 1 |
|

śrīdevyuvāca ||

catuspīṭhasamāyuktaṃ ṣaṭprakārasamanvitaṃ |
vālakramasamopetaṃ śrīkramaṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

yoginīpīṭhamārūḍhaṃ śrīkramaṃ prativādinīṃ |
nityānaṃdakarī devyāṃ śrīdevī praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

saṃsārapāranistāraṃ pārāyaṇamanuttamaṃ |
parāparavibhedajñaṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

āṇavaṃ sāṃbhavaṃ caiva śaktayemiti ca tridhā |
vedhabhaktasya kurvāṇaṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

pratyakṣe ca parokṣe ca sutīvrājñā prabhāvataḥ |
sāmarthyavedhadīkṣāyāṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

p. 3b)

anādijanmasaṃprāptaḥ karmavaṃdhavidāyakaḥ |
jñānānalaprabhāvena śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

ajñāraśmibhirāvṛtya namo hṛtpadma bodhakaḥ |
sarvalokadayā nityaṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ ||

jñānavijñānasaṃpanna animādyaṣṭasiddhidaṃ |
rāgadveṣavinirmuktaṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyahaṃ || 

devyuvāca ||

ehikaṃ muṣmikaṃ kāryaṃ gurubhyo jñāyate sadā |
kiṃ bhūtā guravaḥ svāmi prasādaṃ kartumarhasi ||

ye kecid vicaraṃ tyasmiṃ nācāyyāḥ pṛthivī tale |
teṣāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ nūnaṃ śrotumicchāmi tatvataḥ ||

śiṣyāṇāmapi yaṃ vrūhi lakṣaṇaṃ dīkṣaṇastathā |
samayācāraṇaṃ caiva nirvāṇaṃ ca sadāśiva ||

īśvarauvāca ||

sādhu sādhu tvayā bhadre pṛṣṭhat tatva jighṛcchayā |
sarvaṃ yathā krameṇaiva * * * (?) śruyatāmidaṃ ||

ye veśadaṃbhakālo vārācāryāste prabodhakāḥ | 
ya eva punarācāryā na ca te veśadaṃbhakāḥ ||

paiśunyaṃ krūratāsāṭhyaṃ kṣudrakarma parigrahaḥ |
atīvāhamatībhāvāḥ kauṭilyaṃ vā dhanaṃ tathā ||

atikrodha mahātmādo māyā ca paraniṃdanaṃ |
dravya tṛṣṇā ca mohaṃ ca dhūrtatvaṃ kautukaṃ tathā ||

paropaghāṭīdauśīnyaṃ kṛtti na vyavahārikaḥ |
etairiva guṇairyuktā garhitā nāmadhārakāḥ ||

daṃbhakāstena cācānyā na ca pūjā mahītale |
guru dravyābhilāśī ca yoginī dhanabhakṣakāḥ ||

vīrabhāryābhikāṃkṣī ca svapūjāsthāna vaṃcakāḥ |
prāyaścittī na saṃdeho bhramate yonijecchayā ||

p. 4a)

guruvājñā laṃghakoyaṃ narakaṃ na nivartate |
bhaye na dravya lobhena mohād viṣaya kāṃkṣaya ||

kaścidanyāya tasyaiva māhamārtha prasiddhaye |
dīkṣāvidhi vihīnaṃ tu guṇodbhava vivarjitaḥ ||

svayamevodadatvā tu śrīkramaṃyaprayacchati |
tādṛśaśca gurornityaṃ śrīmānājñāya jīvinaḥ ||

dadato pṛcchato vāpi rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |
nirguṇācārya śiṣya saṃsārādi na cottare ||

bhinnanāvāṃ yathā rājñā tathāparaṃ na gacchati |
evaṃ'duḥkharatopetārācārya kuladaṃbhakāḥ ||

varjayet sadgunā vakṣye śrūyatāṃ kamalānane |
tīvrājñā vedha saṃpannā gurvājñā pratipālakāḥ ||

jñānavijñāna saṃpannā tulāgama visāradāḥ |
samādhi guṇasaṃpannā nitya śrīkramapūjakāḥ ||

nirmoho niṣprapaṃcāśca nitpado niṣparigrahaḥ |
nisādhyo nirahaṃkārī nistṛṣṇo nirvikalpakaḥ ||

kṣamodhānitya saṃtuṣṭassatyavādī jiteṃdriyaḥ |
svahit sa tatparaḥ svaccha sarvajīva dayāparaḥ ||

śāṃtacitta sadācāro dvaṃdva tyāgī dṛḍhavrataḥ |
na strīlolyā na ca krūraḥ kṣudrakarmavivarjitaḥ ||

daṃbhamāyā vinirmuktaḥ krodhalobha vivarjitaḥ |
divyājñānaṃda saṃtṛpta ṛjusādhu subodhakaḥ ||

ācāryamīdṛśaḥ proktastadṛṣṭvā tripradakṣiṇaṃ |
kṛtvā daṃḍa namaskāraṃ kuryāchiṣyaiḥ prayatnataḥ ||

p. 4b)

bhaktiyukta sadākālaṃ kleśaṃ dātmānusāravat |
kṛtvānugrahalābhena gati siddhiṃ samaśnute ||

devyuvāca ||

śrīnātha tvat prasādena jñātamācārya lakṣaṇaṃ |
śiṣyasya lakṣaṇaṃ svāmi prasādaṃ kuru śaṃkara ||

śrībhairava uvāca ||

sādhu pṛṣṭhaṃ tvayā bhadre śiṣyalakṣaṇamuttamaṃ |
kathayāmi samāsena śrūyatāṃ kamalānane ||

dhātukhanya vilākṣetra maṃtrataṃtra rasāyaṇaṃ |
viṣagraharasādau ca vāde yonā'bhikokṣitaḥ ||

anyatra nirapekṣaśca gati siddhirapekṣakaḥ |
saṃsārabhayabhītātmā muktimārgābhilāsakaḥ ||

adhūrtāmatimāṃ śāṃta sāṭhya kautuka varjitaḥ |
anyaṃ ca devatā bhaktā presanārthī priyaṃvaḍaḥ ||

krodhavyasana paiśūnya mithyā vinaya varjitaḥ |
audāraṃ ca paricchedī bhāvyarūpa kṣamāparaḥ ||

śarīra prāṇamarthaṃ ca sadgurubhyo nivedayet |
karmaṇā manasā vācā guruṇā bhaktivatsale ||

mātsaryā nāta nnirmukto nābhyoṃtara parīkṣakaḥ |
śivatatvābhikāṃkṣī ca śiṣye yaṃ kuladīpaka ||

evaṃ bhūtaguṇaṃ śiṣye sa parīṣye prayatnataḥ |
ācāryo dīkṣayennityaṃ su tīvrājñāvalokamaḥ ||

anyathā nirguṇopetaṃ śiṣyaṃ yo dīkṣayed guruḥ |
dadato gṛhnato vāpi kulaśrāpo bhavet sadā ||

devyuvāca ||

svāmi tava prasādena vijñātaśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ |
tasya dīkṣā vidhānaṃ tu vrūhi me parameśvara ||

śrībhairava uvāca ||

sādhu devi tvayā pṛṣṭhaṃ yo dīkṣā vidhitastathā |
tārācakraṃ rāśicakraṃ nāmacakraṃ tathaiva ca ||

p. 5a)

atra cet sa guromaṃtro nānya cakraṃ vicārayet |
viṃśaghadyadhikāmaṃtrāḥ mālāmaṃtrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

napuṃsakasya maṃtrasya siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet |
ekākṣarasya maṃtrasya rālāmaṃtrasya pārvati ||

vaidikasya ca maṃtrasya siddhyādīnnaiva śodhayet |
tāraśuddhi vaiṣṇavānāṃ koṣṭhaśuddhiḥ śivasya ca ||

rāśiśuddhistraipure ca gopāle'kaḍamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
vargacakraṃ pravakṣyāmi jyotirāgamasammataṃ ||

maṃtrāṇāmariśodhārthaṃ nāmādyakṣara pūrvakaḥ |
aṣṭau koṣṭhān samāpādya vargānaṣṭau likhet tataḥ ||

paṃkṣimārjāra paṃcāsya gurusarpākhudaṃtinaḥ |
meṣa kramādakārādi vasuvargāśritā ime ||

sādhyasādhakaḥ yo prītirjñātavyā jātitaḥ sadā |
pakṣimārjārayovairaṃ vairaṃrda kukkura paṃkṣiṇo ||

vairaṃ pakṣi bhujaṃgānāṃ śatruvaṃ gajasiṃhayoḥ |
meṣakukkurayo vairaṃ prītiyuktyā vicārayet ||

evaṃ cakravaraṃ jñātvā gṛhnīyā maṃtramuttamaṃ |
mārjāra kukkuraṣkālaiḥ pakṣiṇāṃ vairitā bhavet ||

mārjārasya śunākhubhyāṃ siṃhasye daṃtinā sadā |
kukkurasya khagābhyāṃ tu kharākhubhyāṃ ca bhogina ||

uṃdūrasyau tu sarpābhyāṃ hariṇā sahadaṃtinaḥ |
bhujaṃga siṃhayormaitrī gajameṣakayostathā ||

lokādanyatra vijñeyaṃ vyavahāra prayogataḥ |
maṃtrāraṃbhastu caitra syāt samasta puruṣārthadaḥ ||

vaiśākhe ratnalābhaḥ syā jyeṣṭhe tu maraṇaṃ bhavet |
āṣāḍhe vaṃdhunāśaḥ syāt pūrṇārthaḥ śrāvaṇe bhavet ||

p. 5b)

prajñā nāśo bhaved bhādre āśvine ratna saṃcayaḥ |
kārtike maṃtrasiddhiḥ syāt mārgaśīrṣa tathā bhavet ||

pauṣe tu śatrupīḍā syāt māghamedhā vivarddhanaṃ |
phālguṇe sarvakāmāḥ syurmalamāsaṃ vivarjayet ||

ravivāre bhaved vittaṃ some śāṃti bhavet kila |
āyuraṃgārakehaṃti tatra dīkṣā vivarjayet ||

budhasauṃdaryamāpnoti jñāmaṃ syāt tu vṛhaspatau |
śukrasaubhāgyamāpnoti yaśo hāniḥ śanaiścare ||

pratipadi kṛtā dīkṣā jñāna nāśakarī matā |
dvitīyāyāṃ bhave jñānaṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ śucirbhavet ||

caturthyāṃ cittanāśaḥ syāt paṃcamī buddhivarddhanaṃ |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ jñānakṣayaḥ saukhyaṃ labhate saptamī dine ||

aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaḥ syāt navamyāṃ puruṣaḥ kṣayaḥ |
daśamyāṃ rājasaubhāgyamekādaśyāṃ śuci bhavet ||

dvādaśyāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ syāt trayodaśyāṃ daridratā |
tiryag yoniḥ caturdaśyāṃ kṛmirmāsāvasānake ||

pakṣāṃte'dharmavṛddhiḥ syāda svādhyāyaṃ vivarjayet |
saṃdhyāgarjita nirghoṣa bhūkaṃpolkānipātanaṃ ||

etānanyāṃśca divasān śrutyuktān parivarjayet |
aśvinyāṃ sukhamāpnoti bharaṇyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvaṃ ||

kartikāyāṃ bhaved dūḥkhī rohinyāṃ pātakī bhavet |
mṛgaśīrṣe sukhāvāptirādrāyāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvaṃ ||

punarvasau dhanāḍhyaḥ syāt puṣpaśatru vināśanaṃ |
aśleṣāyāṃ bhaven mṛtyuḥ maghāyāṃ duḥkhamocanaṃ ||

p. 6a)

sauṃdaryāṃ pūrvaphālguṇyaṃ prāpnoti na ca saṃśayaḥ |
jñānaṃ cottaraphālguṇyāṃ hastāyāṃ ca dhanaṃ bhavet ||

citrāyāṃ jñānavṛddhiḥ syāt svātyāṃ śatru vināśanaṃ |
viśākhāyāṃ sukhaṃ cānurādhāyāṃ vaṃdhuvarddhanaṃ ||

jeṣṭhāyāṃ śatru hāniḥ syāt mūlāyāṃ kīrti varddhanaṃ |
pūrvāṣāḍhottarāṣāḍhe bhavatī kīrti dāyike ||

śravaṇāyāṃ bhavet duḥkhī dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ daridratā |
buddhiḥ śatabhiṣāyāṃ syāt pūrvabhādre sukhī bhavet ||

saukhyaṃ cottarabhādre ca revatyāṃ kīrtivarddhanaṃ |
śubhaśca siddhiścāyuṣmān dhruvayogastataḥ paraṃ ||

prītiḥ saubhāgya yogaśca vṛddhiyogastataḥ paraṃ |
harṣaṇaśca tathā yogāḥ sarva taṃtre sukhāvahāḥ ||

anya yogaṃ pragṛhnīyā viparīta phalapradaṃ |
vavavālataitilaśca vanijastadanaṃtaraṃ ||

karaṇāneti sā proktā sarvataṃtreṣu bhāvini |
dīkṣālagnaṃ pravakṣyāmi vārāhītaṃtra saṃsthitaṃ ||

vṛṣe siṃhe ca kanyāyāṃ dhanumīnākhyalagnake |
caṃdratārānukūle ca dadyādīkṣāṃ sadā guruḥ ||

sthiralagne viṣṇumaṃtraṃ śivamaṃtraṃ caraṃ śubhaṃ |
dvitsvabhāvagataṃ lagnaṃ śaktimaṃtreṣu śasyate ||

dīkṣāsthānaṃ pravakṣyāmi bhairavokta prakāśayet |
gośālāyāṃ gurogrehe devāṃgāre ca kānane ||

puṇyatīrthe tathodyāne nadītīre ca svāśrame |
dhātrī bilva samīpe ca parvatāgre guhāṃśu ca ||

gaṃgāyāṃ tatkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ koṭi koṭi guṇaṃ bhavet |
bhakṣaṃ haviṣyāṃśākādi vihitaṃ ca phalaṃ payaḥ ||

p. 6b)

mūlaṃ sakturyavotpanno bhakṣaṃ netāni maṃtriṇāt |
dīkṣāṃ kṛtvā yadā maṃtrī hyupavāsaṃ samācaret ||

tasya devaḥ sadā ruṣṭaḥ śrāpaṃ patati mūrddhani |
tadā gṛhītānopāṣyedupavāsaḥ śriyaṃ haret ||

tasyādannaṃ samaśrīyāddhaviṣyānnena śāṃkari |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi maṃtroddhāraḥ śṛṇu priye ||

tāro namo bhagavate varāhādrupamuccaret |
bhūpatitvaṃ me dehīti dāpayāṃtegni gehinī ||

trayastriṃśadvarṇakoyaṃ vārāhaḥ kathito manuḥ |
oṃ pārthive ca pṛthivītalavāriṇi vāyumā ||

tatolini ca tāruṇyaṃ kuru yugmaṃ ca dhaṃcadhaṃ |
svāhaikona triṃśadarṇaḥ pārthivāyā manuḥ smṛtaḥ ||

oṃ hrīṁ namaśca vārāhi ghore svapnaṃ visargayuk |
ṭhadvayaṃ vahnijāyāṃto maṃtraḥ paṃcadaśākṣaraḥ ||

tato dhyāyed ghana śyāmāṃ trinetramunnatastanīṃ |
kolāsyāṃ caṃdrabhālāṃ ca daṃṣṭroddhṛta vasuṃdharāṃ ||

khaḍgaikuśaidakṣiṇayorvāmayoścarma pāśakau |
aśvārūḍhāṃ ca kolāsyāṃ nānālaṃkārabhūṣitāṃ ||

raktābjakarṇikā pāṃtu śavāsana samāsthitāṃ |
muṇḍamālā lasatkaṃpaṃ nīlābhāṃ dadhatī karaiḥ ||

abhayaṃ muśalaṃ vāpi phalaṃ cāpi varaṃ tathā |
varāhāsyāṃ tuṃgakuṃcāṃ trinetrā maruṇāṃvarāṃ ||

iti dhyātvā maheśāni śiṣya śirasi siṃcayet |
puṣpaśaṃkhābhiṣekaṃ ca tulā pratyayameva ca ||

rajamaṃḍalavekhā ca dīkṣā paṃcavidhaḥ smṛtā |
śiṣyahastaśataṃ puṣpaṃ svayameva śiraṃbhalaṃ ||

āruhe jñāna sāmarthyāddīkṣā puṣpābhiṣecanam |
hastaṃsthaṃ śaṃkhapānīyaṃ svayamāruhyamastakaṃ ||

p. 7a)

kuryāt tadābhiṣekaṃ ca tathā śaṃkhābhiṣecanaṃ |
tulayā sammitaṃ śiṣyaṃ dīkṣayet tadanaṃtaraṃ ||

laghutve karma vichedāt sa tulapratyaya smṛtaḥ |
likhitvaṣṭa dalaṃ padmaṃ vāhyato vajramaṃḍalaṃ ||

tanmadhye sthāpayecchiṣyaṃ yogiṇī dravyasaṃyutaṃ |
saṃpūjya yoginī vṛṃdaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpe śragādibhiḥ ||

ācāryamabhiṣekastu rajo maṇḍalamucyate |
evaṃ dīkṣā catuṣkaṃ tu mayā taṃtre'psudīritaṃ ||

mūlādhārāt tato vījaṃ brahmamārgeṇa deśikaḥ |
haṃsena puṣkarasthāne paramātmāṇi yojayet ||

vedhadīkṣā varārohe kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
āṇavaṃ prathamaṃ proktaṃ śākte yastu dvitīyakaṃ ||

tṛtīyaṃ sāṃbhavaṃ devi tridhā bhedaṃ prarakṣyate |
yena keṇāpi maṃtreṇa parśe stobha vidhānataḥ ||

kālāṃtare guṇotpattirāṇavaṃśamudāhṛtaṃ |
kuṃḍalādyādi śaktīnāṃ śiṣyāṃge saṃkrameṇa tu ||

tatkṣaṇena guṇotpatti śākteyaṃ saṃprakīrtitaṃ |
darśarāt sparśanaścaiva vāciko mānasastathā ||

evaṃ catuṣprakāreṇa sāṃbhavaḥ samudāhṛtaṃ |
kāruṇyaṃ dṛṣṭimātreṇa śiṣya sadbhaktivatsale ||

sadya eva guṇotpatti vedho darśaniko bhavet |
daṇḍakaṃṭhāsanaṃ pātra ityādi sparśanena tu ||

aṃgasaṃsparśanādvāpi sparśanāvedhamucyate |
samacittaṃ guroḥ śiṣya yadā vijñāyate sadā ||

yat kiṃcid dadate vākyaṃ vedhā bhavati vācikaṃ |
yojanānāṃ śatairvāpi dvitripaṃcaśatepi vā ||

dūrādārādha sadbhaktyā triśuddhyā ca karoti yaḥ |
tasya bhaktiṃ guruṃ jñātvā kāruṇena yadā smaret ||

p. 7b)

tadeva guṇasaṃprāpti vedhomāna sa ucyate |
evaṃ dīkṣāvidhānena śiṣyadīkṣābhilāsakaḥ ||

dīkṣopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ ānīyaṃ gurusannidhau |
sugaṃdhamakṛtaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpadīpa caruṃ tathā ||

bhakṣyabhojya samāyuktaṃ vastrāṇi sa hiraṇyakaṃ |
karpūrapuṣpatāṃvūla bhūṣaṇāṃ vādarāni ca ||

yat saṃbhavati tatsarvaṃ nivedya gurave tadā |
pūjayed yoginī vṛṃdamekacitta samāhitaḥ ||

tataḥ paścāṭ charīreṇa śrīgurubhyo nivedayet |
anyathā vittadīnāpi gurukleśa parāyaṇaṃ ||

kṛtvā daṃḍa namaskāraṃ sa śarīraṃ nivedayet |
dhanāḍhyaṃ dhanadīnāṃ vā triśuddhyā bhaktivatsalaḥ ||

vinītaṃ śiṣyamācārya kāruṇyamavalokayet |
kāruṇyaṃ dṛṣṭimātreṇa śiṣyaḥ patati bhūtale ||

akathya jñānasaṃyuktaṃ vastu śīghramudīryataḥ |
ānaṃdaṃ codbhavakaṃpo nidrāghūrmiśca paṃcamaḥ ||

tatva vidvasya deveśi paṃcāvasthā prakīrtitaḥ |
bhūmityāgaṃstathāveśaṃ mudrāvaṃdhamanekadhā ||

dūrāśravaṇavijñānaṃ sarvabhāṣā pragalbhatā |
yogābhūtaṃ rasotmādo gītaṃ ca haśinaṃ tathā ||

gītaṃ ca vādyamāvādyai svecchā krīḍā pravartake |
pūrvameva guṇot pādo guṇānāṃ śatamudbhavet ||

tadviśra?ma sahasreṇa tallīne lakṣamudbhavet |
tasminneva layo gīte tīvrājñāyā prabhāvataḥ ||

prāpyate paramaṃ sthānaṃ jarāmaraṇa varjitaṃ |
evaṃ dīkṣā vidhānena kṛtvā karmakṣayo guruḥ ||

samastaguṇasaṃprāpte dadyācca śrīkramantataḥ ||

iti śrīvārāhītaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasya dvitīyapaṭalaḥ || 2 ||


p. 8a)

devyuvāca ||

bhagavan tvat prasādena jñātvā dīkṣāvidhi kramaṃ |
śiṣyasyācāraṇaṃ satyaṃ vrūhi me parameśvara ||

śrībhairava uvāca ||

samyageva tvayā pṛṣṭhaṃ śiṣyasyācāra lakṣaṇaṃ |
anukrameṇa deveśi tatsarvaṃ śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ ||

vahujanmāṃtarai pūrvaṃ svakarma kṣayakāriṇaḥ |
dīkṣāmātreṇa śiṣyasya sa bhavet siddhiruttamāḥ ||

yasya janmāṃtarā yastu karmavaṃdhastu vidyate |
na tasya guru kāruṇyaṃ yāvat karmaṃ ca muṃcati ||

yaṃ labdhā guru kāruṇyaṃ śuddhācāro na vidyate |
yadi siddhi tadā naiva tasmādācāra vāgbhavet ||

prātaḥ śrīmaṃḍalaṃ kuryād gaṃdhapuṣpo kṛtādibhiḥ |
kuryād daṇḍa namaskāraṃ gururādeśa pūrvakaṃ ||

ekāṃte nirjanasthāne pūrvasaṃpūjya pustakaṃ |
tatvopadeśasaṃyuktaṃ śṛṇuṣva śrīkulāgamaṃ ||

śrutvāgamopadeśārthamavadhāya prayatnataḥ |
yatpreṣayati tatsarvaṃ kleśakurvītadāsavat ||

śrīmānanyatapoveśī śāstrakrameṇayopi vā |
kleśadaṇḍa namaskāraṃ nalajya gurusannidhau ||

ati hāsyamavaṣṭavyaṃ līlāmaṃgaprasāraṇaṃ |
vinādo jvalaveśaṃ ca vikāraṃ daṃtadhāvanaṃ ||

pādaprakṣālanaśvecchā bhūṣaṇaṃ vādanaṃ tathā |
na kuryād guruso dṛśya yadi siddhimapekṣakaḥ ||

guruṇāmāsanaṃ kaṃṭhā yogadaṇḍaṃ ca pādukau |
akṣamālā ca śayanaṃ kaupīnaṃ vastrameva ca ||

pātra snāna jalaṃ caiva anyonyapakaraṇāni ca |
bhaktimānna ca pādābhyāṃ na spṛśanna ca laṃghayet ||

gaṃdhapuṣpaṃ ca tāmvūlaṃ bhakṣyabhojyāsanādibhiḥ |
nivedya gurave paścād gṛhītā deśapūrvakaṃ ||

p. 8b)

evaṃ kṛtvā guruḥ kleśa śiṣyo niścala bhaktimān |
labhate gurukāruṇyamabhiṣeka catuṣṭayaṃ ||

ājñā pravartanaṃ śiṣye śakāsad guruvājñayā |
svecchayā vicarelloke satataṃ siddhikākṣitaḥ ||

bhikṣānnaṃ deharakṣārthaṃ kaṃṭhāśītātamacchadā |
kharparā bhojanārthaṃ tu gṛhnītātma parigrahaḥ ||

vṛkṣamūle śmaśāne ca parvatāgra guhāṃtare |
śūnyadevālaye vālaye vāpi vased yogīśvarassadā ||

mitrāmitraṃ sukhaṃ duḥkha iṣṭāniṣṭaṃ śubhāśubha |
evamānāpamānaṃ ca tathā niṃdāprasansane ||

kharparaṃ ca hiraṇyaṃ ca kānsalohādanaṃ tathā |
samāni ciṃtayed yogī gati siddhi padīchati ||

kvacid bhaṭṭa kvaciccaṭṭaḥ kvacit pravrajito naraḥ |
svacchārūpadharo yogī pacyate tu mahītale ||

guruṃ ca bhavatīmāṃ ca tathāmnāyaṃ kulāgamaṃ |
maṃtraṃ ca madirā jyeṣṭhāṃ bhaktyāt saṃpūjayet sadā ||

apriyaṃ kṣobhamālasyaṃ ājñābhaṃgaṃ parigrahaṃ |
parastrī laulyamākṣeyaṃ na kuryān muktikāṃkṣiṇaḥ ||

maṃtramudrākṣa sūtrāṇi yogiṇī saṅgamaṃ tathā |
yogavīryaṃ vaṭaṃ pīṭhaṃ siddhadravyāṇi gopayet ||

rāgadveṣa mahaṃkāra paiśunyaṃ veśadaṃbhakaḥ |
krodhaṃ lobhaṃ ca mohaṃ ca hanyānyetāni dāpayet ||

anyeṣāṃ samayācārāṃ kumārī yogiṇīgaṇaṃ |
siddhadravyaṃ ca tatpīṭha paraveṣṭā na niṃdayet ||

yogiṇī siddhacāritraṃ pāraṃparya kramāgataṃ |
adṛṣṭā srūtapūrvaṃ ca śāstra guhya vadena tuḥ ||

mallakurjaṭ yudhaddhaṃ ca nagnastrī paṭatastanā |
sarveṣāṃ carati krīḍā samayagrāhakastathā ||

p. 9a)

jyota nṛtyeṃdra jālāni mṛgayā? vīravigrahaṃ |
kanyā yoniṃ tathāguhyaṃ nekṣayet kulayoginaḥ ||

vṛddhakuṣṭāṃgahīnaṃ ca kīlālaṃ vyādhitaṃ śava |
mātmaviṭ mūtra urgatvaṃ na jugupsayet sarāṃ tathā ||

vṛkṣamūlā latāvallī tṛṇādi sthāvaraṃ sadā |
vinmūlayenna saṃbhidyāt na chidyānna ca pīḍayet ||

gaṃdhapuṣpādikaṃ kiṃcit yogiṇī dravyameva ca |
yadyata prārthitaṃ ladhvaṃ tena sat pūjyate kṣamaṃ ||

tāsāmasaṃbhaved yogī bhāvaśuddhi parāyaṇa |
sadā smaraṇa puṣpeṇa pūjayed gurupādukaṃ ||

kauṭīlyaṃ vādagoṣṭhāṃ ca mātsaryaṃ paśu saṃgamaṃ |
pāśānakāṣṭha mṛtpujā na kuryāt siddhikāraṇaṃ ||

ekāṃte śayanaṃ kuryāt na kuryād dehapīṭhanaṃ |
atyādārasanādānaṃ kṣudra karmāṇi varjayet ||

nānāgamamabhīpseta nāgītaṃ viṣayaṃ smaret |
prāptaṃ pariharet tasmād yasmāt kuryājjiteṃdriyaḥ ||

rocate yatra tatraiva tatra tatraiva gamyate |
manarodhana kartavyaṃ nirvighnaṃ siddhimicchatāḥ ||

ityācāreṇa mārgeṇa satataṃ yaḥ samācaret |
saṃcaraṃtibhyamatyaṃtamanekavidhi siddhibhāk ||

devyuvāca ||

svāmi tvadīya kāruṇyā jñātamācāraṇaṃ paraṃ |
nirvāṇaṃ jñātumicchāmi vaktavyaṃmaparāparaṃ ||

īśvara uvāca ||

sādhupṛṣṭhaṃ varārohe nirvāṇaṃ padamavyayaṃ |
vakṣya parāparaṃ tatvaṃ śūyatāmaṃvujānane ||

samācāraṇamārgeṇa samyarācāraṇassadā |
udbhavaṃti gaṇāssarve tīvrājñā ca pravartake ||

nābhi hṛtkaṃṭha vaktre tu nāsāgre madhyalocane |
karṇāṭ karṇagataṃ tatvaṃ sadodayamakhaṃḍitaṃ ||

p. 9b)

samudraghoṣaṇā dṛśyaṃ kiṃkiṇī ghaṃṭhikā samaṃ |
meghe dhvani nihaṃ tatvaṃ sarvakāramudīrayet ||

gacchatatiṣṭhaśī vāpi jāgrato svapatopi vā |
unmeṣaṃ ca nimeṣe ca śucya vāpya śucāvadhi ||

kṣaṇānave kṣaṇārddhe vā sadāstamanavarjitaḥ |
yathodayaṃ tathā vaṃde vartate ca niraṃtaraṃ ||

sadānaṃdodbhave dehe na nidrā na tṛṣā kṣudhā |
na moho na ca vai dveṣo na rāgo naiva ciṃtanā ||

nāpyaso na ratiścaiva na śītaṃ coṣṇavedanā |
gītaṃ ca hasitaṃ nṛtyaṃ vādyamānedyameva ca ||

dhāvanaṃ laṃghanaṃ caiva svecchālāpa pravartate |
evamādi vikārāṇi saṃpadyaṃte yathā tathā ||

tathā tathā na bhetakyaṃ krīḍate vasudhā tale |
ityevaṃ paramānandamunmattākāra ceṣṭitaṃ ||

dṛṣṭvā na jñāninassarve hasaṃte puruṣottamaḥ |
śāstramārgā yathā mūrkho kumārī strī sukhaṃ yathā ||

tathā ca khalu pāpistho yogamārga hasaṃti ca |
jñānino lokacāritraṃ bhavatyunmatta ceṣṭitaṃ ||

lokikairapi taṃ jñānaṃ unmattariva dṛśyate |
loke niṃdāpraśaṃsaṃti na jānaṃ krīḍanaṃ sadā ||

parānaṃda svarūpeṇa śivaināmaikarūpakaiḥ |
ātmeti ca śarīrastha svāhṛta paramaṃ padaṃ ||

dūtī gamāgamaṃ sarvaṃ jānīyād yoga tatparaḥ |
ātmatatvamidaṃ jñātvā paratatve vimelayet ||

tatradgatīkṣayennityaṃ labhate siddhimakṣayaṃ |
na snānaṃ na japo homo na dhyānaṃ na ca dhāraṇā ||

na mudrā nāśanaṃ caiva na śusumnā na ca piṃgalā |
kuṃbhakāre ca ke naiva pūrakamya vidhinna ca ||

na tithinna ca nakṣatraṃ nopavāsa vratādikaṃ |
tatsarvaṃ bhava vaṃdharthaṃ na karmakṣayakāraṇaṃ ||

p. 10a)

sa prapaṃca nibhā tyajya niṣprapaṃca samācaret |
muktāvasthaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vyāmohe naiva vādhyate ||

icchāvyāmoha nirmukto mukti svābhāvikā bhavet |
na buddhirna manaścaiva na ca paṃceṃdriyāṇi ca ||

na bhūmi na sthalaṃ nāgnirnna ca vāyu na cāṃvaraṃ |
matamonarajaḥ satvo na prapyaṃ prerakanna ca ||

na ca dūraṃ na cāsannaṃ na kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ na ca |
na kṣayaṃ vṛddhimāyātaṃ na varṇāvarṇabhedakaṃ ||

evamādī vidhirmuktaṃ akṣayaṃ paramaṃ padaṃ |
tilamadhye yathā tailaṃ kāṣṭhamadhye yathānalaṃ ||

pāṣāṇe ca yathā hemaṃ kṣīramadhye yathā ghṛtaṃ |
tathā sarvagatatatve dehe dehe vyavasthitaṃ ||

mūḍhacittā na jānaṃti paraṃ sūkṣma niraṃjanaṃ |
anyathā tarka ṣaṭkaṃ tu śāstratābhyaryamanyathā ||

anyathā vedamārgārthaṃ anyathā gamaśāsanaṃ |
anyathā lokikaṃ mārgaṃ anyathā pāramārthikaṃ ||

tasmād bhrāṃti tyajed yogī nirvikalpaṃ samācaret |
ṣaṭtriṃśa tatva nirmuktaṃ paṃcapaṃcaka varjitaṃ ||

caṃdranaṣṭa kalātītaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃta vivarjitaṃ |
na cānyātītamatyantaṃ dvādaśāṃtāṃtaraṃ paraṃ ||

sarvātmā sarvagannityaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvato mukhaṃ |
akhaṇḍita ravicchinnamaprameyamanāhataṃ ||

niṣkalaṃ nirmalaṃ śāṃtaṃ nirācāra pade sthitaṃ |
anaṃtamakṣayaṃ sūkṣmaṃ rūpātītaṃ parāparam ||

evaṃ śiva svarūpeṇa siddhi tyaṣṭvāni modayaḥ |
dūrāśravaṇavijñānaṃ dūrādarśananena ca ||

chāyāvirahi tatvaṃ ca parakāya praveśanaḥ |
icchārūpaṃ bhaven nityaṃ iti siddhi pravarttate ||

p. 10b)

svayaṃ guruḥ svayaṃ śiṣyaṃ svayaṃ śakti svayaṃ śivaḥ |
svayaṃ sūkṣma svayaṃ sthūlaḥ svayameva parāparaṃ ||

ārādhyānaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ maṇḍalestu kṛte sati |
nāriktamaṃḍalaṃ kuryād yadicchet siddhiruttamāṃ ||

yadi lobhaṃ kṛte mūḍhariktamaṃḍalakaṃ guroḥ |
maṃtraśrāpo bhavet tasya yoginī jananiṃdakāḥ ||

dravyādīnāmalābhena svadehaṃ ca nivedayet ||

iti śrīvārāhītantre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasya tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || 3 
||

bhairava uvāca ||

dīkṣitaśca śukau deśe guru śukrādaye śubhe |
lagne copacayasthāpi vihīne pāpavikṣaṇai ||

puṇyamāsepya divase śubharaktā vivarjite |
śubhagrahādi saṃyukte puraścaraṇa mā labhet ||

kārtikāścina dvaiśākhe māghetha mārgaśīrṣake |
phālguṇe śrāvaṇe dīkṣā puraścaraṇa śasyate ||

ravau gurau śite māse kartavyaṃ budhavāsare |
candratārānukūle ca puraścaraṇa karma tat ||

rohiṇī śravaṇādrā ca dhaniṣṭhā cottarā trayaṃ |
puṣyaśatabhiṣāhastā puraścaraṇa karmaṇi ||

pūrṇimā paṃcamī caiva dvitīyā saptamī tathā |
trayodaśī ca daśamī praśastā sarvakarmasu ||

sattīrtheka vidhugrāse taṃtu dvaya na parvaṇe |
puraścaryā prakurvāṇo māsakṣādīnna śodhayet ||

maṃtrādyāraṃbhanaṃ meṣa dhanadhānya pradaṃ bhavet |
vṛṣamaraṇa saṃprāpti mithune jalpanāsinī ||

karkaṭe sarvasiddhiḥ syāt siṃhe medhā vināśakaḥ |
kanyā lakṣmī tadā nityaṃ tulāyā sarvasiddhayaḥ ||

vṛścike svarṇa lābhaṃ syāt dhanujñāna vināśakaḥ |

p. 11a)

makaraḥ putradaḥ proktaṃ kuṃbhodhanasamṛddhitaḥ ||

mīne duḥkha prado nityaṃ maivaṃ rāsiphalaṃ bhavet |
caṃdratārānukūle ca śuklapakṣe śubhehani ||

ārabhet tu puraścaryā harau supte navā caret |
puṇyakṣatrādikaṃ gatvā kuryād bhūmi parigrahaṃ ||

tathā cāmukamaṃtrasya puraścaraṇa siddhaye |
mayaiva gṛhyate bhūmi maṃtroyaṃ siddhyatāmiti ||

nagarādāvapi krośaṃ kroṇayugmamathāpi vā |
āhārādi vihārārthaṃ tāvatīṃ bhūmimā kramet ||

kṣīravṛkṣodbhavān kīlān astramaṃtrābhimaṃtritān |
nikhaned deśa digbhāge teṣvastraṃ ca prapūjayet ||

lokapālān purasteṣu gaṃdhādyaiḥ pūjayet sudhī |
kṣetrapālādikaṃ tatra pūjayed vidhivat tataḥ ||

kṣatraśaṃvāstu nāmānaṃ vighnarājaṃ samarcayet |
dikpatibhyo valiṃ dadyāt tataḥ kṣetraṃ samaviśet ||

ye raudrā raudra karmāṇo raudra sthāna nivāsinaḥ |
mātaropyagrarūpā ca gaṇādhipatayeścaye ||

vighnabhūtāścayaṃ cānye digvidikṣu samāśritāḥ |
sarvate prīti manasaḥ pratigṛhnaṃ tvimaṃ valiṃ ||

prātaḥ snātvā tu gāyatryāḥ sahasraṃ prayato japet |
jñātā jñātasya pāyasya kṣamārthaṃ prathamaṃ tataḥ ||

hrīṁ brahmāyaṇī vidmahe caturdalāyai dhīmahi || tanno devī pracodayāt || 
māheśvaryai vidmahe gaurī priyāya dhīmahi || tannoścaṇḍī pracodayāt || kaumārī 
ca vidmahe viṃdusahitāya dhīmahi || tanno devī pracodayāt || viṣṇuśakti vidmahe 
raudramukhāya dhīmahi ||

p. 11b)

tanno kālī pracodayāt ||

vārāhi ca vidmahe ghoramukhī dhīmahi tanno kālī pracodayāt || iṃdrāyaṇī vidmahe 
indraśakti dhīmahi || tannośakra pracodayāt || guhyakālī vidmahe guptaśakti dhīmahi 
tanno kālī pracodayāt || mahālakṣmī vidmahe mahāśakti dhīmahi || tanno śrīya 
pracodayāt || 

jayā ca vijayayā caiva jayaṃtī cāparājitā |
naṃdā bhadrā jayā bhīmā kālī caivāṣṭakāstathā ||

divyayogni mahāyogni siddhiyogni gaṇeśvarī |
sākiṇī kāla rātrī ca ūrddhvakeśī niśācarī ||

gambhīrāghoṣaṇī sthūlā pavanāṃgī ulīnitā |
bhīṣanā ca mahānaṃdā jvālāmukhī tathaiva ca ||

piśāci hastinī caiva yakṣiṇī dhūrjatīstathā |
viṣabhakṣa sarvasiddhistuti icchā tathaiva ca ||

hūṁ kālī bhāśvarī dīrghā dhūmrākṣi karaha priye |
mātaṅgī kāka dṛṣṭī ca tathā tripura cāṃtakī ||

rākṣasī ghoranādāśca raktākṣi viśvarūpiṇī |
bhayaṃkarī raudravībha śrīśuṣkāṃganarabhojanī ||

vīrabhadra mahākālī kaṃkālī vikṛtānarā |
krodharā bhīmabhadrāśca vāyuvegā bhayānanā ||

brahmā ca vaiṣṇavī raudrī carcikā īśvarī tathā |
vārāhī nārasiṃhī ca śivadūtī catuṣṭikā ||

idaṃ devyā mahāyogniṃ valiṃ gṛhnaṃ tu sarvadā |
na kuryācchāktikī dīkṣāmuparakte vibhāvasau ||

na kuryāṃ vaiṣṇavīnāṃ tu yadi candramaso grahe |
anyeṣu puṇyayogeṣu grahaṇe caṃdrasūryayoḥ ||

sūryagrahaṇakāle tu nānyadatvepi taṃ bhave ||

p. 12a)

śiṣyanāhūya guruṇā kṛpayā yadi dīyate |
tadā lagnādikaṃ kiṃcina vicāryaṃ kathaṃcana ||

sarve vārāgrahāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi na saṃśayaḥ |
yasminna hani saṃtuṣṭo guruḥ suśruṣahācahāḥ ||

grahaṇe ca mahātīrthe nāsti kālasya nirṇayaḥ |
gayāyāṃ bhāskarakṣatre viraje caṃdraparvate ||

vaṭule ca mataṃge ca tathā kanyāśrameṣu ca |
na gṛhnīyāt tato dīkṣāṃ tīrtheṣvateṣu pārvati ||

śukrāsto yadi vā vṛddho gurvādityo bhaved yadi |
meṣavṛścika siṃheṣu tadā doṣo na vidyate ||

kalamālā pravakṣyāmi vārāhītaṃtra ukta ca |
tarjanī madhyamā nāmā kaniṣṭhā cetatāḥ kramāt ||

tisrogulyastriparvāṇo madhyamā caika parvikā |
parva dvayaṃ madhyamāyā merutvenopa kalpayet ||

anāmā madhyamārabhya kaniṣṭhādita eva ca |
tarjanī mūlaparyaṃtaṃ daśaparvasu saṃjapet ||

anāmāmūlamārabhya kaniṣṭhādi na eva ca |
tarjanī madhya paryaṃtaṃmaṣṭaparvasu saṃjapet ||

anāmikā trayaṃ parva kaniṣṭhādi triparvikā |
madhyamāyāśca tritayaṃ tarjanī mūlaparvaṇi ||

tarjanyagre tathā madhye yojayet sa tu pāpakṛt |
anāmā madhyamārabhya kaniṣṭhādita eva ca ||

tarjanī mūlaparyaṃtaṃ daśaparvasu saṃjapet |
parvadvayaṃ mano māyāḥ parivartena vai kramāt ||

parvatrayaṃ madhyamāyāstarjanyekaṃ samāharet |
śaktimālā samākhyātā sarvataṃtra pradīpikā ||

anāmā mūlamārabhya pradakṣiṇa krameṇa ca |

p. 12b)

madhyamā mūlaparyaṃtaṃ japedaṣṭa suparvasu ||

anāmikā dvayaṃ parva kaniṣṭhādi krameṇa tu |
tarjanī mūla paryaṃtaṃ kalamālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

aṃgulīṃta vidyutāta kiṃcidā kuṃcite tale |
aṅgulīnāṃ viyogācca chidre ca sravate japa ||

aṅgulyagre ca paryaṃtaṃ yajjaptaṃ meru laṃghane |
parvasaṃdhiṣu yajjaptaṃ tatsarvaṃ niḥphalaṃ bhavet ||

gaṇanā vidhimullaṃghya ya japet tajjapaṃ janaḥ |
gṛhnaṃti rākṣasāstena gaṇayet sarvadā vudhaḥ ||

hṛdaye hastamāropya tiryak kṛtvā kalāṃgulaiḥ |
āchādya vā samāhastau dakṣiṇena sadā japaḥ ||

kramāt kramagatairmālā mātṛkārṇaiḥ samerukaiḥ |
sa viṃdukaiḥ sāṣṭavargauḥ raṣṭauvargā prakīrtitāḥ ||

padmavījādibhirmālāḥ vahiryāge śṛṇuṣvatāḥ |
rudrākṣa padmasaṃkhyākhyā putra jīvaka mauktikaiḥ ||

sphāṭikairmaṇi ratnaiśca sauvarṇairvidrumaistathā |
rājataiḥ kuśamūlaiśca gṛhasthasyākṣamālikā ||

aṃgulī gaṇanādekaṃ parvaṇāṣṭa guṇaṃ bhavet |
putrajīvaid daśaguṇaṃ śataṃ śaṃkhaiḥ sahasrakaiḥ ||

pravālairmaṇi ratnaiśca daśasahasrekaṃ smṛtaṃ |
tadvaṃdha sphaṭikaiḥ proktaṃ mauktikairlakṣamucyate ||

paścākṣai daśalakṣaṃ syāt sauvarṇaiḥ koṭirucyate |
kuśagraṃthyā koṭi śataṃ rudrākṣaiḥ syāmanaṃtakaṃ ||

sarvaviracitāmālā nṛṇāṃ muktiphalapradā |
padmākṣe racitā mālā śatrūṇāṃ nāśinī matā ||

kuśagraṃthi mayī mālā sarvapāpa praṇāśinī |
putra jīva phalai kṛptā kurute putra saṃpadaṃ ||

p. 13a)

nirmitārūpyamālābhiḥ japamālepsita pradā |
pravālairvihitā mālā prayached vipulaṃ dhanaṃ ||

suvarṇamaṇibhirmālāṃ sphaṭikī śaṃkhanirmitaṃ |
pravālaireva vā kuryāt putrajīvaṃ ca varjayet ||

padmākṣaṃ caiva rudrākṣaṃ bhadrākṣaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
mahāśaṃkha mayī mālā jñeyā nīla sarasvate ||

nṛlalāṭāsthi khaṃḍena lacitā japamālitā |
mahāśaṃkha mayī mālā tārā jyeṣṭhā jape priyā ||

karṇanetrāṃtare devi mahāśaṃkha prakīrtitā |
raktacaṃdanamālā tu bhogamokṣa pradā bhavet ||

vaiṣṇave tulasīmālā gajadaṃte gaṇeśvare |
tripurāyā jape śastā rudrākṣai raktacaṃdanaiḥ ||

anyonya samarūpāni nāti sthūlaṃ kṛśāni ca |
kīṭādibhiraduṣṭānitajīrṇāni navāni vai ||

paṃcāśatmanibhirmālā vihitā sarvakarmasu |
akārādi kṣakārāṃtāṃ varṇamālā prakīrtitā ||

kṣīrṇameru mukhaṃ tatra kalpayen munisattama |
anayā sarvavidyānāṃ japaḥ sarvasamṛddhidaḥ ||

śmaśāne dhuttūrairmālā jñeyā dhūmāvatī vidhau |
narāṃgulyasthibhirmālā grathitā sarvakāmadā ||

nātryasaṃgrathanaṃ kāryaṃ rektena vāsasā api |
sadā gopyā prayatnena jananyā jāravat priye ||

nityaṃ japaṃ kare kuryāt na tu kāmyaṃ kadācana |
kāmyamapi kare kuryā mālā'bhāvena suṃdari ||

mamāsenākṣa sūtrasya vidhānamiha kathyate |
paṃcaviṃśatibhirmokṣaṃ triṃśadbhirdhanasiddhaye ||

sarverthoḥ saptaviṃśatyā paṃcadaśyābhicālake |
paṃcāśadbhiḥ kārya siddhiḥ syāt tathā caturuttaraiḥ ||

p. 13b)

aṣṭottara śataiḥ sarve siddhirukto manīṣibhiḥ |
kāmyārthaṃ kaṃvalaṃ caiva sreṣṭaṃ ca raktakaṃvalaṃ ||

kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhiḥ mokṣa śrīvyāghracarmaṇi |
kuśāsane maṃtrasiddhi nātrakāryākāryāvicāraṇā ||

dharaṇyāṃ duḥkhasaṃbhūti daubhāgyaṃ dārujāsane |
vaṃśāsane daridra syāt pāṣāṇe vyādhi pīḍanaṃ ||

tṛṇāśane yaśo hāni pallavecitta vibhramaḥ |
japahāni tapo hāni vastrāsanaṃ karoti hi ||

naracarmāsanaṃ devi vārāhyā maṃtra siddhaye |
mahāmālā mahāpātraṃ mālāmaṃtrastu pārvati ||

vajravārāhikā devi durlabhaṃ tridaśairavi |
daśasaṃskāra maṃtrāṇāṃ mahāmālāstu sādhanaṃ ||

adhunā kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇutvaṃ kamalānane |
chinnādi doṣa duṣṭāyaṃ manavaḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ ||

satataṃ sevyamānāste navasaṃti phalapradā |
atastad doṣanāsāya saṃskārān daśavakṣyahaṃ ||

trikoṇa saptadhā maṃtrī vibhajyāt samabhāgataḥ |
īśakoṇāt tathā kuryāt tathā pāvaka koṇataḥ ||

unapaṃcāśa devasyuryonayastatra sarvataḥ |
īśānyadilitmaṃtrī vāruṇāṃtaṃ lipiṃ kramāt ||

atra devīṃ samabhyarcyo maṃtrān sarvasamuddharet |
etajjananamityāhuratho dīpa na mucyate ||

puṭitaṃ haṃsamaṃtreṇa sahasraṃ saṃjapen manuṃ |
idaṃ dīpanamākhyātaṃ bodhanaṃ tannigadyate ||

varmaṇārehayuktena puṭitaṃ saṃjapen manuṃ |
sahasraiḥ paṃcabhirmaṃtrī vodhanaṃ saṃsmṛtaṃ tvidam ||

p. 14a)

astra saṃpuṭitaṃ maṃtraṃ sahasraṃ prajapet sudhīḥ |
etat tāḍanamākhyātamabhiṣeka udīryate ||

vāk haṃsa praṇavairmaṃtrī sahasraṃ saṃjapejjalaṃ |
imaṃ maṃtraṃ paṭhen maṃtramabhiṣiṃcyājjalaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||

tāvān srauṁmitimaṃtreṇa vaṣaḍaṃtena saṃpuṭaṃ |
sahasraṃ saṃjapen maṃtrī vimalī karaṇaṃ tvidaṃ ||

svadhā śikhā puṭaṃ maṃtraṃ sahasraṃ saṃjapet sudhīḥ |
etajjīvanamākhyātaṃ dugdhājyamilitairjalaiḥ ||

tarpaṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ khyātaṃ māyayā saṃpuṭo manuḥ |
sahasraiḥ paṃcabhirjapto gopanaṃ tatsamucyate ||

madhunāśakti maṃtre tu vaiṣṇave veṃdumajjalaiḥ |
ghṛtena tarpaṇaṃ śaive maṃtrībhiḥ samyagīritaṃ ||

śvetacaṃdrānugraheṃdu saṃpuṭaṃ prajapet manuḥ |
etadāpyāyanaṃ prāktaṃ maṃtraśāstra viśāradaiḥ ||

saṃproktā daśasaṃskārā maṃtrārṇā taṃtra coditaḥ |
māle māle mahāmāle sarvatatvā svarūpiṇī ||

caturvargastvayinyastastasmāne siddhidā bhava |
mālā vījādikaṃ kṛtvā raktapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet ||

vāgbhavaṃ ca tathā lakṣmīṃmakṣādi mālikāṃ tataḥ |
ṅetāṃ hṛdaya varṇāṃtāṃ maṃtreṇānena pūjayet ||

oṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ hrīṁ klīṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || 

iti maṃtreṇa saptadhā'bhimaṃtrya ca karṣe niḥkṣipet ||

taṃ taḥ āvāhanādi maṃtrādibhirbhagavatīmāvāhya saparivārāṃ 
ṣoḍaśopacārairabhyarcya mūlamāvartya tārtīyaṃ vījamanusmaran |
dhenumudrayā'mṛtīkṛtya kavacenāvaguṇṭhya mahāmudrayā tadā chādayan 
devī rūpītāṃ mālī dhyāyan viṃduprekṣayaṃ kuryāt tatra maṃtraḥ ||

p. 14b)

oṃ hrīṁ svāhā oṃṁ hūṁ svāhā || oṃ phaṭ svāhā || oṃ strīṁ svāhā || oṃ hrīṁ 
hūṁ svāhā || oṃ hrīṁ phaṭ svāhā || anena daśa tathā viṃdu prakṣepaṃ kuryāt || 
kastūrikā guru sudhākarairdhūpādikaṃ datvā ca kaṣāduttārya ebhirmaṃtrayet || 

oṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ mahāvajriṇi mahāghorarūpe karkaśa mahāsthimaṇḍale praviśa 
sarvasiddhiṃ prayaccha oṃ hūṁ hrīṁ svāhā mahākapālini mahāghorarūpe svāhā 
|| oṃ hrīṁ hrīṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā dūrvākṣatairabhyarcyakaṃ darpavījenoddīpanaṃ 
kṛtvā ṣoḍaśopacārairabhyarcya mātṛkāmaṃtreṇa saṃdīpya hūṁkāreṇa 
veṣṭayitvā valiṃ dadyāt tadyathā || oṃ hrīṁ hrīṁ hūṁ hūṁ oṃ phaṭ hūṁ oṃ 
oṃ hūṁ svāhā oṃ hūṁ mahāyogini tribhuvanatāriṇi mahāśaṃkhāsthi 
mālāmadhye nivāsaṃ kuru 2 sarvasiddhiṃ dehi dehi surāmāṃsopahārān gṛhna 
gṛhnāpaya hrīṁ strīṁ phaṭ svāhā || anena yathodita dravyeṇa valiṃ datvā devīṃ 
visṛrjya nibhṛta sthāne mālāṃ dhārayediti || 

aṣṭottara sahasraṃ tu kṛtvāṃ taryāgamādarāt |
japet pratidinaṃ yat tu nitya eṣa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

mūlādhārāt tato vījaṃ brahmamārgeṇa deśikaḥ |
haṃsena puṣparasthāne paramātmāṇi yojayet ||


iti śrīvārāhītaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasya caturthapaṭalaḥ || 4 ||

bhairava uvāca ||

gurorājñāṃ samādāya śuddhāṃtaḥ karaṇo naraḥ |
tataḥ puraskriyāṃ kuryā maṃtrasiddhi samīhayā ||

p. 15a)

jīvahīno yathā devī sarvakarmasu nakṣamaḥ |

p. 15a)

puraścaraṇa hīinopi tathā maṃtraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||

tasmādādau svayaṃ kuryād guru vā kārayed budhaḥ |
gurorabhāve vipraṃ vā sarvaprāṇi hiterataṃ ||

snigdhaṃ maṃtravidaṃ mitraṃ nānāguṇasamanvitāṃ |
ādau puraskriyāṃ kartuṃ snāna niyama ucyate ||

puṇyakṣetraṃ nadītīraṃ guhāparvatamastakaṃ |
tīrthapradeśāḥ siṃdhūnāṃ saṃgamaḥ pāvanaṃgamaṃ ||

udyānāni viviktāni bilvamūlataṭaṃ śireḥ |
tulasīkānanaṃ goṣṭha vṛṣaśūnyaṃ śivālayaṃ ||

aśvatthamalakī mūlaṃ gośālā jala madhyataḥ |
devatā yatanaṃ kūlaṃ samudrasyā nijaṃ gṛhaṃ ||

gṛhe śataguṇaṃ vidyād goṣṭhe lakṣaguṇaṃ bhavet |
koṭi devālaye puṇyaṃ manaṃta śivasannidhau ||

mlekṣa duṣṭamṛgavyādha śaṃkāṭaka vivarjite |
ekāṃte pāvane niṃdā rahite bhakti saṃyute ||

svadeśe dhārmike deśe subhikṣe nirupadrave |
ramyabhaktajanasthāne nivaset tapasa priye ||

guruṇāṃ sannidhāne ca citte kāgre sthale tathā |
etāmanyatamasthāne māśritya japamārabhet ||

yatra grāme japet maṃtrī tatra kūrmaṃ viciṃtayet |
parvate siṃdhū tīre vā puṇyāraṇye nadī taṭe ||

yadi kuryāt puraścaryā tatra kūrmaṃ viciṃtayet |
grāme vā yadi vā rāṣṭra gṛhaṃ taṃ ca viciṃtayet ||

puraścaraṇa kṛtmaṃtrī bhakṣyā bhakṣyaṃ vicārayet |
anyathā bhojanā doṣāt siddhihāniḥ prajāyate ||

dadhikṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ gavyaṃmaikṣavaṃ guḍa varjitaṃ |
tilāścaiva sitā mudgā kaṃdaḥke mūlavarjitaḥ ||

p. 15b)

nārikelaphalaṃ caiva kadalī lavalī tathā |
āmramāmalakaṃ caiva panasaṃ ca harītakī ||

prakārāṃtare haviṣyaṃ ca praśastaṃmanyathā budhaiḥ |
bhujyano vā haviṣyānnaṃ śākayāvakameva vā ||

payomūlaṃ phalaṃ vāpi yatra yatropalabhyate |
vījāṃ ca nālikāśākaṃ kalāyaṃ ca nakaṃ tathā ||

kadaṃvaṃ nālikelaṃ ca vrate kuṣmāṇḍakaṃ tyajet |
vivarjayet madhukṣāraṃ lavanaṃ tailameva ca ||

tāṃvūlaṃ kāṃśyapātraṃ ca divā bhojanameva ca |
tadā drākṣāṃ ca lavanaṃ māṃsaṃ saṃgṛṃjana bhojanaṃ ||

māṣāḍhakī masūrāśca kodravāṃścanakānapi |
kauṭilyaṃ kṣauramatyaṃgaṃ anivedita bhojanaṃ ||

asaṃkalpita kṛtyaṃ ca varjayen mardanādikaṃ |
snāyācca paṃcagavyena kevalāṃ malakena vā ||

maṃtrajānānna pānīyaiḥ snānā ca mana bhojanaṃ |
kuryād yathokta vidhinā trisaṃdhyaṃ devatārcanaṃ ||

apavitrakaro nagraḥ śirasi prāvṛtopi vā |
pralepat prajaped yāvat tāvan niḥphalamucyate ||

yasmānnayāna puṣṭāṃgaḥ kurute dharmasaṃcayaṃ |
annadātu phalasyārddhe kartuścārddhana saṃśayaḥ ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena parānnaṃ varjayet sudhīḥ |
puraścaraṇa kāle tu sarvakarmasu śāstravit ||

jihvādagdhaparānne ca karau dagdhau pratigrahā |
manodagdhaṃ parastrībhiḥ kathaṃ siddhirvarānane ||

vaidikācāra yuktānāṃ śucīnāṃ śrīmatāṃ satāṃ |
satkulasthānajātānāṃ bhikṣāśī cāgra janmanāṃ ||

sakṛśca durite śabde praṇavaṃ samudīrayet |
prākte pārśve hare śabda prāṇāyāmaṃ sa kṛccaret ||

p. 16a)

vahupralāpīcācasya nyāsyāṃgāni tato japet |
kṣute pyetaṃ tathā'spaśya sthānānāṃ sparśanepi ca ||

evamādīśca diyamān puraścaraṇa kṛccaret |
viṇmūtrotsargasaṃkādi yuktaṃ karma karoti yaḥ ||

japārcanādikaṃ sarvamapavitraṃ bhavet priye |
malināṃ varakeśādi mukhadaurgaṃdhya saṃyutaḥ ||

yo japet satahastāśu devatā guptisaṃsthitā |
ālasya jaṃbhanaṃ nidrāṃ kṣutaṃ niṣṭhīvanaṃ bhayaṃ ||

nīcāṃga sparśanaṃ kopaṃ japakāle vivarjayet |
evamukta vidhānena vilaṃvaṃ tvaritaṃ vinā ||

uktasaṃkhyaṃ japaṃ kuryā puraścaraṇa siddhaye |
devatā guru maṃtrāṇāṃmaikyaṃ saṃbhāvayet dhiyā ||

japadekamanāḥ prātaḥkālaṃ madhyaṃdināvadhi |
yatsaṃkhyā yā samālabdhaṃ tatkartavya maharniśaṃ ||

yadi nyūnādhikaṃ kuryād vratabhraṣṭo bhaven naraḥ |
yatsaṃkhyayā samārabdhaṃ tajjaptavyaṃ dine dine ||

nyūnādhikaṃ na kartavyaṃ asamāptaṃ sadā japet |
nyūnāti riktakarmāṇi na phalaṃti kadācana ||

yathāvidhi kṛtānye ca tatkarmāṇi phalaṃti hi |
bhūśayyā brahmacāritvaṃ maunaṃ cācārya sevanaṃ ||

nityapūjā nitya dānaṃ devatā stuti kīrtanaṃ |
naimittikārcanaṃ caiva viśvāso guru devayoḥ ||

japaniṣṭhā dvādaśaite dharmāsyu maṃtrasiddhidaḥ |
strīśūdrapativrātya nāstiko chiṣṭabhāṣaṇaṃ ||

asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ jihvā bhāṣaṇaṃ parivarjayet |
satyanāpi na bhāṣeta japahomārcanādiṣu ||

anyathāṃ tuṣṭitaṃ sarvaṃ bhavatyeva nirarthakaṃ |
śayātakuśaśayyāyāṃ śucivastradharaḥ sadā ||

p. 16b)

pratyahaṃ jālaye śayyāmekākī nirbhayaḥ svapet |
asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ vācaṃ kuṭilāṃ parivarjayet ||

varjayed gītavādyādi śravaṇaṃ nṛtya darśanaṃ |
atyaṃgaṃ gaṃdhalepaṃ ca puṣpādhāraṇameva ca ||

tyajedusnodaka snānaṃ anyadeva prapūjanaṃ |
naikavāsā japen maṃtraṃ vahu vāsā kulopi vā ||

patitā nāmaṃ tyajānāṃ darśane bhāṣaṇe śrute |
kṣute'dho vā yugamane jṛṃbhane japamutsṛjet ||

tathā tasya ca tatprāptau prāṇāyāma ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ |
kṛtvā samyak japoccheṣaṃ yadvā sūryādi darśane ||

manaḥ saṃharaṇaṃ śaucaṃ maunaṃ maṃtrārtha ciṃtanaṃ |
avyagratvamanirvedo japasaṃpatti hetavaḥ ||

uṣṇīṣī kaṃcukī nagno muktakeśo ganā vṛtaḥ |
apavitrakarā'śuddhaḥ praleyaṃta japet kvacit ||

anāśanaḥ śayāno vā gacchan bhuṃjāna eva vā |
apavṛttau karau kṛtvā śirasā prāvṛtopi vā ||

ciṃtā vyākulacitto vā krūddho bhrāṃtaḥ kṣudhānvitaḥ |
nīthyāyāma śivasthāne na jayaṃti mirāṃtare ||

upānadguḍhapādo vādyāna śayyā gatastathāḥ |
prasārya na japet pādo vutkaṭāsana eva ca ||

mārjāraṃ kukkuṭaṃ kroṃcaṃ śvānaṃ śūdra kapiṃ kharaṃ |
dṛṣṭvā campa japet samyak spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhāyate ||

aśūcirvāśucirvāpi gacchan tiṣṭhan svapannati |
maṃtraika śaraṇo vidvān manasaiva sadācaret ||

na doṣo mānase japye sarvadeśepi sarvadā |
japaniṣṭho dvijaḥ śreṣṭho'khila yajñaphalaṃ japet ||

sarveṣāmeva yajñānāṃ jāyate'sau mahāphalaiḥ |
japena devatā nityaṃ śrūyamānā prasīdati ||

p. 17a)

prasannā vipulān kāmān dadyān mukti ca śāśvatīṃ |
yakṣarakṣaḥ piśācādyāḥ grahāsarpāḥ vabhīṣaṇāḥ ||

jāpinaṃ tāya sarpaṃti bhayabhītā samaṃ tataḥ |
yāvaṃ taḥ karmayajñāḥ syupratiṣṭhāni tapāṃsi ca ||

sarve te japa yajñasya kalānāhaṃti ṣoḍaśīṃ |
mahāmyaṃ vācikasyai tat japayajñasya kīrtita ||

tasmācchata guṇo pāśuḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
mānasaḥ siddhikāmena puṣṭikāmairupāśukaḥ ||

vāciko māraṇe caiva praśasto japa īritaḥ |
śāktyā śeṣavanaṃ snānaṃ anyathā dve sakṛccavā ||

trisaṃdhyaṃ prajapen maṃtraṃ pūjanaṃ tatsamaṃ bhavet |
ekadāvā bhavet pūjā na japet pūjanaṃ vinā ||

japāṃte vā bhavet pūjā pūjāṃte vā japet manuṃ |
prātaḥkālaṃ samārabhya japen madhyaṃ dināvadhi ||

manaḥ saṃhṛtya viṣayāt maṃtrārthagatamānasaḥ |
na drutaṃ na vilaṃvaṃ ca japen mauktikahā bhavet ||

japaḥ syādakṣarāvṛttirmānasossa? pāṃśuvāvikaiḥ |
dhiyācārṇasya paṃkti vai varṇasvara padātmikāṃ ||

uccaredarthamuddiśyaḥ mānasaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |
jihvoṣṭau cālayet kiṃcid devatā gatamānasaḥ ||

kiṃcicchravaṇayogyaḥ syā upāṃśuḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |
maṃtramuccārayedvācā vācikaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

nijakarṇā gocaro yaḥ mānasaḥ sa japaḥ smṛtaḥ |
upāṃśu nijakarṇasya gocaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||

nigadastu janairvedyastrividho japa itiritaḥ |
uccai japād viśiṣṭaḥ syāt upāṃśurdaśabhirguṇaiḥ ||

jihvā japāchataguṇaiḥ sahasro manasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
uccairjapo'dhamaḥ proktaḥ upāṃśurmadhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

p. 17b)

uttamo mānaso devi trividhaḥ kathito japaḥ |
ati hrasvo vyādhi hetu ati dīrgho vapukṣayaḥ ||

akṣarākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ japenmauktikahāravat |
manasā yaḥ smaret stotraṃ vacasā vā manuṃ japet ||

ubhayaṃ niḥphalaṃ yāṃti bhinnabhāṇḍodakaṃ yathā |
paśubhāve sthitā maṃtrāḥ kevalāvarṇarūpiṇaḥ ||

sausumnadhyanyaccaritāḥ prabhutvaṃ prāpnuvaṃti te |
maṃtrākṣarāṇi vichaktau proktāni paribhāvayet ||

tāmeva paramavyomni paramāmṛtavṛṃhite |
darśayitvāt samudbhāvaṃ pūjā homādibhircana ||

manonyatra śivonyatra śaktiraṃnyatramārutaḥ |
na siddhyati varārohe kalpakoṭi śatairapi ||

jātasūtakamādau syādaṃte ca mṛtasūtakaṃ |
sūtaka dvaya saṃyukto yo maṃtraḥ sanasiddhyati ||

gurostadrahitaṃ kṛtvā maṃtrayāvadyayoddhiyā |
sūtaka dvaya saṃyuktaḥ sa maṃtraḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ ||

brahmavījaṃ manau datvā cādyaṃta parameśvari |
saptavāra japet maṃtraṃ sūtaka dvaya muktaye ||

maṃtrārthamaṃtra caitanyaṃ yonimudrā navektitaḥ |
śatakoṭi japenāpi tasya siddhinnajāyate ||

luptavījāścaye maṃtrāḥ nadāsyaṃti phala priye |
maṃtrāścaitanya sahitāḥ sarvasiddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||

caitanyarahitā maṃtrāḥ proktā varṇāstu kevalāḥ |
phalaṃ naiva prayacchaṃti lakṣakoṭi japairapi ||

maṃtroccārai kṛtai yādṛk svarūpaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet |
śatasahasralakṣe vā koṭi jāpyena yatphalaṃ ||

hṛdaye graṃthi bhedaśca sarvāvayavavarddhanaṃ |
ānaṃdā śṛṇi pulako dehāveśaḥ kuleśvari ||

p. 18a)

gaṅgadoktiśca sahasā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
sakṛduccaritepyevaṃ maṃtracaitanya saṃpuṭe ||

dṛśyate pratyayā yatra pāraṃparya taducyate |
māsamātraṃ japetyeva bhūtalipyā tu saṃpuṭaṃ ||

kramat kramāt sahasraṃ tu tasya siddho bhavet manuḥ |
paṃcahrasvāḥ saṃdhivarṇāḥ vyomarogniyelaṃdharāḥ ||

aṃtyamādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca caturthamadhyamaṃ kramāt |
paṃcavargākṣarāṇi syurcāṃte sveteṃdubhiḥ sahaḥ ||

etā bhūta tvipiḥ proktā dvicatvāriṃśadakṣaraiḥ |
evaṃ japaṃ purā kṛtvā tejorūpaṃ samarpayet ||

devasya dakṣiṇe haste kuśapuṣpārya vāribhiḥ |
saphalaṃ tadvibhāvyaiva prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret ||

japasyādau japāṃte ca tritayaṃ tritayaṃ caret |
evaṃ japaṃ purā kṛtvā gaṃdhākṣata kuśodakaiḥ ||

japaṃ samāpayed devyā vāmahaste vicakṣaṇaḥ |
japāṃte pratyahaṃ maṃtrī homayet tad daśāṃśataḥ ||

tarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣekaṃ ca tad daśāṃśaṃ jape priye |
pratyahaṃ bhojayed viprān nyūnādhika praśāṃtaye ||

anyathā sarvapūrṇe ca homādikamathācaret |
yadyagaṃdaṃ bhavedbhaṃgaṃ tatsaṃkhyā dviguṇo japaḥ ||

homābhāve japaḥ kāryo homasaṃkhyā caturguṇaḥ |
viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca rasasaṃkhyāguṇaiḥ smṛtaḥ ||

vaiśyānāṃ vasusaṃkhyākameṣāṃ strīṇāmayaṃvidhiḥ |
yaḥ varṇamāśritaḥ śūdraḥ sa ca tasya vidhiṃ care ||

śūdrasya vipra bhṛtyasya tatpatnyāḥ sadṛśo japaḥ |
homakarmaṇya śaktānāṃ viprāṇāṃ dviguṇo japaḥ ||

p. 18b)

itareṣā tu varṇānāṃ triguṇādi samanvitaḥ |
praṇavoccāraṇāddhomāt śāragrāmaśilārcanāt ||

brāhmaṇī gamanāccaiva śūdraścāṃḍālatāṃ vrajet |
aṣṭākṣaro mahāmaṃtraḥ saptārṇastu striśūdrayoḥ ||

praṇavādiścayo maṃtraḥ na strīśūdre praśasyate |
yadyadaṃgavihīyeta tatsaṃkhyā dviguṇo japaḥ ||

kartavyaścāṃgasiddhyarthaṃ tadaṃśakte na bhaktitaḥ |
na cedaṃga vihīnena tadviśiṣṭamavāpnuyāt ||

yadi homena śaktaḥ syāt pūjāyāṃ tarpaṇepi vā |
tāvat saṃkhya japennaiva puraścaraṇamācaret ||

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt bhojanā chādanādibhiḥ |
gurusaṃtoṣamātreṇa maṃtrasiddhirbhaveddhruvaṃ ||

samyak siddhyaika maṃtrasya paṃcāṃgopāsanena ca |
sarve maṃtrāśca siddhyaṃti tatprasādāt kuleśvari ||

gurumūlamidaṃ sarvamityāhustaṃtravedinaḥ |
ekagrāmasthito nityaṃ gatvā vaṃdeta vai guruṃ ||

gurureva parabrahma tasmādādautamarcayet |
tadaṃte mahatīṃ pūjā kuryāt sādhakasattamaḥ ||

suvāśinīṃ kumārīṃ ca bhūṣaṇairapi bhūṣayet |
miṣṭānnaṃ vahusaṃkāryaṃ bhuṃjīta sahavaṃdhubhiḥ ||

evaṃ siddhamanurmaṃtrī sādhayet sakalepsitān |
athavānya prakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate ||

grahane'rkasya ceṃdorvā śūciḥ pūrvamukhoṣitaḥ |
nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ nābhimātrodake sthitaḥ ||

sparśādvimukti paryaṃtaṃ japenmaṃtraṃ manaṃnyadhīḥ |
yadvā puṇyodake snātvā śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ ||

grahaṇādi vimokṣāṃtaṃ japenmaṃtraṃ samāhitaḥ |
athavānya prakāreṇa pauraścaraṇako vidhiḥ ||

p. 19a)

caṃdrasūryo parāgena snātvā prayatamānasaḥ |
sparśādi mokṣaparyaṃtaṃ japenmaṃtramananyadhīḥ ||

dṛṣṭvā snātvā susaṃkalpo vimokṣāṃtaṃ japaṃ caret |
japād daśāśato homaḥ tathā homastu tarpaṇaṃ ||

evaṃ kṛtvā tu maṃtrasya jāyate siddhiruttamā |
tāvad yajñādikaṃ kuryād grahaṇāṃte śuciḥ pumān ||

iti japān maṃtraṃ siddhi bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
śāddhādairanurodhenadi jāpyaṃtatyajet naraḥ ||

sa bhaved devatā drohī pitṛn saptanayatyadhaḥ |
kalpokta vidhinā maṃtrī kuryāddhomaṃ samāhitaḥ ||

athavā tad daśāṃśena homādīṃśca samācaret |
anaṃtaraṃ daśāṃśena kramāddhomādikaṃ caret ||

tadaṃte mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ |
tato maṃtra prasiddhyarthaṃ guruṃ saṃpūjyatoṣayet ||

evaṃ ca maṃtrasiddhiḥ syād devatā ca prasīdati |
japo homastarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣeko viprabhojanaṃ ||

paṃcāṃgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamucyate ||

iti vārāhītaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasya paṃcamodhyāyaḥ || 5 ||


bhairava uvāca ||

pūrṇābhiṣekaṃ vakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ śubhāvahaṃ |
vināyenābhiṣekena sādhakaḥ pūrṇa bodhataṃ ||

ācāryatvaṃ na cāpnoti sadgatiṃ ca samāhitaṃ |
tasmād guruḥ priyaṃ śiṣyaṃ bodhayitvābhiṣecayet ||

viracya vipulaṃ cakraṃ maṇḍape'ti manohare |
khārītoyabhṛtaṃ kuṃbha sthāpayeccakra madhyataḥ ||

anyeṣu kalaśāstatra vastra hemasamanvitān |
daleṣu vidhivat sthāpya tatrāvāhyaṣṭadevatāṃ ||

p. 19b)

abhyarcya madhye cānyeṣuṃ cāgā varaṇa devatāṃ |
śiṣyasya janmanakṣatre prāgvattairabhiṣecayet ||

nānopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya sahasraṃ juhuyācchuco |
abhiṣiṃced guruḥ śiṣye rāmāsyai pratiśet svayaṃ ||

pūrṇābhiṣekaḥ pāto yamācāryatva pradāyakaḥ |
ayaṃ pūrṇābhiṣekaḥ syāt sādhakābhiṣṭa siddhidaḥ ||

paladvayaṃ tu prasṛti kuḍavaṃ dviguṇam mataṃ |
caturbhiḥ kuḍavaiḥ prastha āḍhakaiśca tu gugguṇaiḥ ||

caturguṇo bhaved droṇaḥ kuṃbhastadvayataḥ smṛtaḥ |
pūrṇābhiṣeka hīnastu kauliko śriyate yadi ||

piśācatva mavāpnoti yāvadā bhūtasaṃplava |
tīrthāsanaṃ ca saṃnyāsaṃ vratadhāraṇameva ca ||

upavāsaṃ muṃḍanaṃ ca sarvathā parivarjayet |
pūrṇābhiṣeke śirasi tena tatra na muṃḍanaṃ ||

kāmarūpāśca caṃpākānepālotthāśva saiṃdhakaḥ |
vāgāgāṃdhārapau liṃgāḥ savaite cādhamādhamā ||

vīrāṇāṃ kaulikānāṃ tu bhartā dīkṣā samācaret |
bhartu dīkṣāpradāne tu na doṣaḥ kālikāgame ||

śivena dīkṣāpūrvaṃ tu goryai dattā śubhāvahā |
sā ca putryathavā bhāryā tadvadatrāpi pārvati ||

na putrī yadi tāsā tu vīrabhāryā prakīrtitā |
adīkṣitakṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ śrāddhaṃ cādīkṣitasya vā ||

gṛhitvā pitarasteṣāṃ narake ca sudāruṇe |
patatyavana saṃdeho yāvadriṃdrāścaturdaśaiḥ ||

vahupratigrahāśakta ācārya śiṣyayāvaha |
svanāmnā na gurukāryo bhāryā ca mātṛnāmikā ||

devī deva viśeṣānī karmakṛtvā sumaṃtriṇā |
mahāvalipradānena prasṛte deśamaṃḍale ||

p. 20a)

vāmamārge caturdikṣu maṇḍalasya valiṃ caret |
brahmasthāne tu vetālo meṣastasya valiṃ surā ||

viṣṇusthāne bhairavīsyadajastasyāvaliṃ mataḥ |
bhairavastu śivasthāne māhiṣaṃ valimicchati ||

gauḍimādhvī ca paiṣṭhī ca surāstatra kramāt matāḥ |
matsyamāṃsaṃ madyayuktaṃ bhūtebhyo valilikhyate ||

pitṛbhyaḥ sāsavapalaṃ yakṣebhyaḥ kukkuṭo madhuḥ |
bhoktāduṃdu valirnāge vāmamārge valiṃstvayaṃ ||

tṛṇapallavakāṣṭhāni pāṣāṇāṃ mṛṇmayaṃ tathā |
vaṃśāsanaṃ ca vastraṃ ca kevalaṃ kṣitimāsanaṃ ||

ekādaśārṇonenoktā kapālārcā tṛtīyakā |
māyā strī hūṁ svarga kapālāya sarvāpadaṃ vadet ||

devī kapālāya namaścaturtho maṃtra īritaḥ |
ṣaṭpaṃcāśatkavarṇoyaṃ kapālasya tu pūjane ||

tataḥ stusmiṃ kapāle tu pūjayedarkamaṃḍalaṃ |
sūryamaṃḍalāye namoṃtoyaṃ navākṣaraḥ ||

sūryamaṃḍala pūjāyā nṛkapāle manūrmataḥ |
kūrcayugmaṃ mahākālaṃ prasīdeti padadvayaṃ ||

lajjāyugmaṃ vahnijāyā saṃyuktaḥ ṣoḍaśārṇakaḥ |
koṭikālānalābhāsāṃ caturbhuja trilocanaṃ ||

śmaśānāṣṭaka madhyasthaṃ muṃḍāṣṭaka vibhūṣitaṃ |
paṃcapretaḥ sthitaṃ devaṃ triśūlaṃ ḍamaruṃ tathā ||

khaḍgaṃ ca kharparaṃ caiva vāmadakṣiṇayogataḥ |
vibhrataṃ suṃdaraṃ devaṃ śmaśānabhasmabhūṣitaṃ ||

nānāśavaimuṃḍamālaṃ kālikā hṛdayasthitaṃ |
lālayaṃtaṃ ratāsaktaṃ ghoracuṃvana tatparaṃ ||

gṛddhagomāyutaṃ yuktaṃ pheravīgaṇa saṃyutam |

p. 20b)

jaṭāpaṭala śobhāḍhyā śmaśānyālayacesthitaṃ ||

sarvaśūnyaṃ muṇḍabhūṣaṃ prasannavadanaṃ śivaṃ ||

trailokyanātha jagadīśa maheśvarastvaṃ brahmāṇḍa koṭi jaṭharādbhūta 
śāṃtyacintya |
yastvaṃ mayālpamatinā dvibhujādi dehī dhyāto parādhaśatakaṃ mamatakṣamasva 
||

tvaṃ sūryacaṃdradahaneśa gaṇeśaviṣṇubrahmendraśaktimarayoganagasvarūpaḥ |
dhyātau śivālma viṣaye paripūjitosi tanme parādhaśatakaṃ mama tatkṣamasva ||

vedopi naiva munayo na ca bhāratītvāṃ jānāti maṃdamatimāsvarūktarītyā |
dhyātastvamevamaparādhakṛtā mayedaṃ duḥkhaṃ pralabdha madhunā 
śaraṇāgatoyaṃ ||

dhyānaṃ karomi manasā na ca te hyanaṃta maṃtrākṣarātmakamidaṃ 
paribhāvayāmi |
rūpaṃ tvadīyamiti yatpravadaṃti vedāḥ siddhirjapānna ca vahiḥ kṛtapūjanādyaiḥ 
||

maṇivaṃdhādadhāṃ *? pādāṃgulyādadhaḥ śive |
mukhaṃ prakṣālayed devī vīrasnānamidaṃ bhavet ||

tathā snānaṃ na me deho na ca pāpaṃ mamāsti vai |
kiṃ snānaṃ kasyacit snānaṃ masnātaḥ kṛta bhojanaḥ ||

sadā devyudakaiḥ snāyāt tadabhāve yathodakaṃ |
hakāraḥ śivarūpatvā ukāraḥ pāpanāśakaḥ ||

īkāraḥ kāmarūpatvāt māyā tena prakīrtitā |
vajra satva bhave viṃdu arddheṃdu rocanestathā ||

reṣā ratnādhipaścaiva hakāraścāmṛtaṃyuta |
ūkāraṃ meghasiddhi syāt jñātvā paṃcajinātmakaṃ ||

śmaśāna bhairavīndevīmugratārāṃ tathaiva ca |
ucciṣṭa bhairavī caṇḍīḥ tārātripurasuṃdarīṃ ||

vajravārāhikā devī vetālavāhana smṛtaḥ |

p. 21a)

yaṃ caturthāpi vījena ṣaṣṭhasvara vibhūṣitaḥ ||

agnivetālavījoyaṃ sarvatra bhayanāśakaḥ |
yat pūjayet tasya maṃtra bhūtādityo bhayaṃ na hi ||

dvibhujaḥ pītavadano raktanetra bhayaṃkaraḥ |
duṃvikān dakṣiṇe pāṇau vāme rudhira pātrakaṃ ||

daṃṣṭrā karāla vadanaḥ kṛśodhamaniśaṃ tataḥ |
jaṭāṃ dīrghāpūri vibhrata ghorā vaputastathā ||

mohinī uvāca ||

sādhanāṃ vīrakavacaṃ na jānāmi maheśvara |
kathaṃ maṃtra vidhiṃ kṛtvā īśvarastvaṃ mayā prabhu ||

śrī ānaṃdadevovāca ||

kavacaṃ śṛṇu pārvatyā mahākāla svarūpiṇīṃ ||

asya śrīvetāla kavacasya śrīvīravetāla ṛṣiḥranuṣṭupchaṃda bhūtanātho devatā 
ḍākinī śākinī śakti sarvārtha maṃtra siddhaye viniyogaḥ ||

vīradhvaja vāmahaste dāhināpātrahastake |
tat devā bhojaye nityaṃ surāmāṃsārata priye ||

mahākāla svarūpā ca kālarūpāya dhīmatā |
anena kavacaṃ śrutvā yaḥ paṭhet sādhakottamaḥ ||

oṃ hsauṁ pātu mastakānāṃ parabrahma prakāśaka |
lalāṭe bhairavī pātu ekākṣaraṃ tu nāsike ||

kaṃṭhe pātu viśālākṣī hṛdayakāla pātu māṃ |
phreṁ pātu dvibhuje caiva huṃpātu nābhimadhyake ||

mahāvīrapātu udaraṃ liṃge rakṣāṃ mahānidhi |
mūlādhāraṃ vighnarājo pādamadhya tu mālikaṃ ||

rakṣa rakṣa mahāvīra pātu pādāṃgulī pada |
oṃ hsauṁ mahāvīra 2 vetāla sarvakāryeṣu saṃpada ||

nāmamaṃtraśrutaṃ loke sarvaśatru vidāhaya ||

p. 21b)

haṁ haṁ haṁ bhīmarūpaṃ jvalati śikhi śikhā vīranāthaṃ namāmi |
saṁ saṁ saṁ śatrughāṭa phaṇi phaṇi vibhuṣaṃ pātumāṃ vīranātham ||

śrīṁ śrīṁ śrīṁ kālarūpaṃ vighinivighnaśaṃ kālarūpaṃ namāmi |
vaṁ vaṁ vaṁ vetālanāmaṃ sa ṣaṭ ṣaṭ bhairavo vāhanānāṃ ||

oṃ oṃ oṃ vīranādaṃ viṣaviṣavināśaṃ bhūmikaṃ pāyamānaṃ |
phrīṁ phrīṁ phrīṁ vāyuvegaṃ gata gata gaditaṃ nāda hūṁ kāla netraṃ ||

kaṁ kaṁ kaṁ kālakūṭa praṇita phaṇi phaṇi bhūṣitaṃ sarva agre |
taṁ taṁ taṁ tāḍanādaṃ paṭa paṭa patitaṃ haṃnyate haṃnya pāpaṃ ||

ghaṁ ghaṁ ghaṁ gītavādya gha ghu ghu ru ghu ghura śabdahāsyate vīranāthaṃ |
paṁ paṁ paṁ pātrahastaṃ nararudhiraṃ bhogaṃ rakṣamāṃ vīranāthaṃ ||

iti kavacamajñātvā yo bhajet vīrasādhanaṃ |
śṛṇu pūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā kālopacāramucyate ||

saṃtuṣṭo vīravīrohaṃ pītarūpaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ |
tāraṇaṃ māraṇaṃ devī uccāṭaṃ mohanaṃ tathā ||

caturvidyā bhavet śaktī sādhako dīyate padaṃ |
prakāśe śamasāre vā jalamadhyavanāṃtare ||

kālakukkuṭavīrasya tathā lubdhabhayaṃkaraṃ |
bhojapatre likhe bhakti dāhinā vāhu mūlake ||

sarvaśāstrakavīrāṇāṃ jihvā'gravīra āgate |
vīraprākarmasāmarthaṃ niścaye dīyatetsudhīḥ ||

oṃ hūṁ phaṭ mahāvīra vetālāya vidmahe bhairavāsanāya dhīmahi tanno vīra 
pracodayāt ||

patākā dhvajadaṃḍādaussutaṃ vastraṃ prapūjayet |
anyatra caraṇādau ca tadvināśacyutopi ca ||

raktaṃ kauśeya vastraṃ ca mahādevyai prapūjayet |

p. 22a)

pītaṃ nīlaṃ ca vai dadyāt kauśeyaṃ keśavāya ca ||

raktaṃ ca kaṃvalaṃ dadyā chivāya paramātmane |
vicitra sarvadevebhyoḥ devāṃśukaṃ nivedayet ||

cūrṇakṛto vā ghṛṣṭe vā dāhākarṣita eva vā |
resa saṃsargayo vāpi praṇaṃ gorbhavameva vā ||

gaṃdhaḥ paṃcavidhaḥ prokta devānāṃ prīti dāyakaḥ |
gaṃdhacūrṇaṃ gaṃdhapatraṃ cūrṇaṃ sumanasaṃ tathā ||

praśasta gaṃdha cūrṇānāṃ yatra cūrṇī nipāni tu |
tāni gaṃdhāddha?yāniḥ syuḥ sa gaṃdhaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

ghṛṣṭāmalayajo gaṃdha śaralaśca na meruṇā |
aguru prabhṛtīṃ cāpi yasya paṃkaḥ pradīyate ||

pṛṣṭotha pṛṣṭagaṃdhoyaṃ dvitīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
devadāru guruḥ brahmadānasārā tu caṃdana ||

priyodīnāṃ ca yo dagdhā gṛhyate dāhato rasaḥ |
sa dāha karṣito gaṃdhaṃ tṛtīye parikīrtitaṃ ||

sugaṃdha karavīrāsugaṃdhinī tila * taraṃ |
prabhṛtīnāṃ raso yosmai niḥpīḍya parigṛhyate ||

samasyaṃ mudbhavo gaṃdhaḥ sarvagaṃdha itīṣyate |
mṛganābhi samudbhūtastagrātodbhava eva ca ||

gaṃdhaḥ prāśyaṃ gajaḥ proktā modadaḥ svargavāsināṃ |
karpūragaṃdhasārādyā pakvaghṛṣṭa ca saṃsthitaḥ ||

caṃdrabhāgādayaścāpi rasai pakvarasadgatāḥ |
gaṃdharāraṃ tu sarvatra sarmadādau prapūjyate ||

mṛganābhi bhaved ghṛṣṭaścūrṇo yasyaiva yogataḥ |
evaṃ sarvastu sarvatre gaṃdho bhavati paṃcadhā ||

p. 22b)

pṛṣṭādi bhāvādenyānyaṃ gaṃdhaḥ prītikaraḥ paraḥ |
gaṃdhasya vistaro bhedaḥ prokta kālīpakāṃdayaḥ ||

sarvapaṃcavidhaireva pratiṣṭho bhavati kṣaṇāt |
gaṃdhāmalayajo yastu daiva pitre vasurmata ||

tatvaṃ kovāsaro vāpi cūrṇavaviṣṇustuṣṭidaḥ |
sarveṣu gaṃdhajāteṣu praśasta * mayodbhavā ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena devā malayajaḥ sadā |
pūrṇā guru sa karpūra hito malayajod bhavai ||

vaiṣṇavī prītido gaṃdha kāmākhyāyāśca bhairava |
kuṃkumāguru kastūrī caṃdabhāgaiḥ samīkṛtaḥ ||

tripurā prītito gaṃdhastathā ca dvivaśaṃbhunā |
devatā devatāḥ proktā gaṃdhāt saṃpūjya sādhakaḥ ||

devāyaṣṭāyavibhave sarvasiddhipradaṃ sadā |
gaṃdha na labhate kāmān gaṃdho dharmapradaḥ sadā ||

arthānāṃ sādhako gaṃdho gaṃdhāmokṣaḥ pratisthitaḥ |
ayaṃnau kathito gaṃdhaḥ putrau vetāla bhairavau ||

śāraṃ vā vādanaṃ vastraṃ jīrṇaṃ malinameva vā |
upabhuktaṃ canādadyādvartikārthaṃ ca sādhakaḥ ||

upadadyāt mūlameva satetaṃ śrī vivṛddhaye |
krodhajaṃ rāmajaṃ vastraṃ vartikārthaṃ navācaret ||

dīpavṛkṣāśca kartavyāstraijasādyastha bhairava |
vṛkṣā pradīpo dātavyāratu bhaumau kadācana ||

sarvaṃ sahāvasumatī sahatenannirtha dvayaṃ |
svakā ca pādaghātaṃ ca dīpatāpaṃ tathaiva ca ||

p. 23a)

nādatte tvaṃ janaṃ devī vaiṣṇavī vidhavā kṛtaṃ |
na mṛtpātrayojaye ca sādhako netralaṃjanaṃ ||

na pūjāphalamāpnoti mṛtpātre vihitāṃjanaiḥ |
caturvargaprado dhūpaḥ kāmadaṃ netraraṃjanaṃ ||

tasmādvāramidaṃ dadyād devebhyo bhaktito naraḥ |
āmikṣā paramānaṃ ca dadhi vāpi * śarkaraṃ |

mahādevyai nivedyai ca vājimedhalasaṃbhavet |
mūlakaireṇa māṃsena lohapātra susaṃskṛtaṃ ||

vyaṃjanaṃ gaṃdhasaṃyuktaṃ devīlokamavāpnuyāt ||

iti śrīvārāhitaṃtre dakṣiṇāmnāyanirvāṇapade mahātirahasya ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ || 
6 ||
bhairava uvāca ||

vetālādi mahāsiddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavati caṇḍike |
tanme kathaya deveśi yadi snehosti māṃ prati ||

śrībhairavyuvāca ||

mama hasta sthito nātha mamājñāṃ paripālaya |
evamaṃtryastu vetālaṃ yatra yatra pramucyate ||

deśo nadī gayā śaila gaṃgānarmadapuṣkaraṃ |
vārāṇasī kurukṣetraṃ prayāgaṃ jaṃvukeśvaraṃ ||

keḍāraṃ vāmapādaṃ ca puḍavaṃ puṣkarāhvayaṃ |
someśvaraṃ mahāraṇyaṃ tathā ca marakaṃṭhakaṃ ||

kālaṃ jalaṃ tathā viṃdhyaṃ yatra vāso guhasya ca |
brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate ||

yasmin deśe yadācāraḥ pāraṃparya kramāgataḥ |
varṇānāṃ sāṃtarālānāṃ sa sadāraha ucyate ||

kurukṣetraṃ ca matsyāśca pāṃcālāḥ śūrasainikā |

p. 23b)

eṣa brahmarṣi deśo vai brahmavartādanaṃtaraḥ ||

matsyā cirāgaḥ pāṃcālāḥ kānyakubjāḥ śūrasenikā | 
himavad viṃdhyayormadhye yat prāgvinaśanādapi ||

pratyage ca prayāgācca madhyadeśa udāhṛtaḥ |
āsamudrā tu vai pūrvā dāsamudrāt tu paścimāt ||

tayorevāṃtaraṃgiryorāryāvartaṃ vidurvudhāḥ |
kṛṣṇasārastu caratiṃ mṛgo yatra svabhāvataḥ ||

sa jñeyo yajñi yo deśe mleccha deśa * * sutaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrāścaiva dvijottama ||

arcayaṃti mahādevaṃ yajñadāna samādhibhiḥ |
vratopavāsaniyamairhomasvādhyāya tarpaṇaiḥ ||

teṣāṃ vai devi sāyujyaṃ sāmīpyaṃ cāti durlabhaṃ |
sa lokatāṃ ca sārūpyaṃ jāyate matprasādataḥ ||

snātai pramuditairhṛṣṭai brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairnṛpaiḥ |
vaiśyaiḥ śūdrairbhaktiyuktai mlecchairanyaiśca mānavaiḥ ||

gṛhītvau duṃvaraṃ pātraṃ vāri pūrṇa mudaśmukhaḥ |
upavāsaṃ tu gṛhnīyād yadvāsaṃkalpayed vudhaḥ ||

abhuktvā prātarāhāraṃ snātvā camyasamāhitaḥ |
sūryāya devatābhyaśca nivedya vratamācaret ||

kṣamāsatyaṃ dayādānaṃ śaucamiṃdriyanigrahaḥ |
devapūjāgni havanaṃ saṃtoṣasteyavarjanaṃ ||

sarvavrateṣvayaṃ dharmaḥ sāmānyo daśadhā sthitaḥ |
krodhāt pramādāllobhādvā vratabhaṃgo bhaved yadi ||

dinatrayaṃ na bhuṃjīta muṃḍanaṃ śirasotha vā |
asakṛjjalapānācca sa kṛttāṃvūlacarvaṇāt ||

p. 24a)

upavāsaḥ praṇaśceta divāsvāpācca maithunāt |
aṃtyaye cāṃvupānena nopavāsaḥ praṇasyati ||

ikṣucāpapayo mūlaṃ tāṃvūlaṃ ca mahoṣadhaṃ |
bhuktvā caiva tu kartavyaṃ snānadānādika kriyā ||

rajasvalāṃ ca caṃḍālaṃ mahāpātakinaṃ tathā |
sūtikāṃ patitaṃ caiva ucchiṣṭa rajakādikaṃ ||

vratāni madhye śṛṇuyād yadyeṣāṃ dhvanimuttamaḥ |
aṣṭottara sahasraṃ tu japed vai vedamātaraṃ ||

saṃdhyā hīno śucirnitya manarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yadanyat kurute kiṃcinna tasya phalamaśnute ||

adho vāyu samutsarge prahāsenatabhāṣaṇe |
mārjāramūṣakasparśe ākuṣṭe krodhasaṃbhave ||

nimitteṣveṣu sarvatra karma kurvannayaḥ spṛśet |
snānaṃ saṃdhyātarpaṇādi japahomāmarārcanaṃ ||

upavāsa vratākāryaṃ sāyaṃ saṃkāhutīrvinā |
garbhiṇī sūtikādiśca kumārī cātha rogiṇī ||

yadā śuddhā tadānyena kāraye * yatā svayaṃ |
bhāryā patyurvrataṃ kuryā bhāryāyāśca patirvrataṃ ||

asāmarthye patiṇābhyāṃ vratabhaṃgo na jāyate |
upavāse svaśaktastu āhitāgnirathāpi vā ||

putrādvākārayedanyādbrāhmaṇādvāpi kārayet |
upavāsaṃ prakurvītaḥ puṇyaṃ śataguṇaṃ labhet ||

nārī ca ti * muddiśya devibhaktamupoṣitā |
puṇyaṃ śataguṇaṃ yā yā dityāha bhairavoditaḥ ||

kāmye pratinidhirnāsti nityainaimittikepi ca |
kāmyepyupakramādūrdhvaṃ kecit pratinidhiṃ viduḥ ||

p. 24b)

nasyā prātinidhirmaṃtra svāmi devāgni karmasu |
sa deśakālayornāsti tāraṇeragni cetasā ||

haviṣyeṣu yavā mukhyāstadanuvīhayasmṛtā |
māṣakodravagorādīn sarvābhāvepi varjayet ||

vrīhi ṣaṣṭhi ca mudgāśca kalāyāḥ salilaṃ payaḥ |
śyāmākāścaiva nīvārāḥ godhūmādyā vratebhitāḥ ||

kūṣmāṃḍālāvukāvārtā pālakyajyotsnikāstyajet |
carubhakṣaṃ saktuvāṇāḥ śokaṃ dadhighṛtaṃ madhuḥ ||

śyāmākāśśālinīvārāḥ pāvakaṃ mūlataṃḍulaṃ |
haviṣye vratanaktāhāvagnikāryādike hitaṃ ||

madhumāṃsaṃ vihāyāṃ nyadvrate ca hitamīritaṃ |
ṣaṣṭhikā kālaśākaṃ ca mūlakaṃkemuketarat ||

kaṃdaḥ saindhava sāmudre gavye ca dadhisarpiṣī |
payo'nūdhṛtasāraṃ ca panasāmlaharītakī ||

pippalījīrakaṃ caiva nāgaraṃgakatiṃkiṇī |
kadalī lavalī dhātrī phalānyaguḍasaikṣava ||

suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ muktārājavartapravālakaṃ |
ratnaṃ paṃcakamākhyātaṃ śeṣaṃ vastu vravīmyahaṃ ||

kanakaṃ kulisaṃnīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca mauktikaṃ |
etāni paṃcaratnāni ratnaśāstra vido vidu ||

abhāve sarvaratnānāṃ haima sarvatra yojayet |
muktāphalaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca vaidūryaṃ padmarāgakaṃ ||

puṣparāgaṃ ca gomedaṃ nīlaṃ gārunmataṃ tathā |
pravāla yuktānyuktāni mahāratnāni vai nava ||

aśvattho duṃvaraplakṣa cūtanyadyodhapallavāḥ |
paṃcabhaṃgā iti proktā sarvakarmasuśobhanāḥ ||

p. 25a)

gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhisarpi kuśodakaṃ |
nidṛṣṭaṃ paṃcagavyaṃ ca pavitraṃ kāya śodhanaṃ ||

gāyatryādāya gomūtraṃ gaṃdhadvāreti gomayaṃ |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||

tejosi śukramityājyaṃ devasyatvā kuśodakaṃ |
ebhistu paṃcabhiryuktaṃ paṃcagavyaṃ pracakṣate ||

paṃcāmṛtaṃ dadhikṣīraṃ sitā madhughṛtastathā |
madhurāmlaṃ ca lavaṇaḥ kaṣāyastikta eva ca ||

kaṭukaśceti he devi rasa ṣaṭkamudāhṛtaṃ |
kastūrikāyā dvau bhāgau catvāraḥścaṃdanasya ca ||

kuṃkumasya trayaścaikaṃ śaśinaḥ syāccatuḥ samaṃ |
kuṣṭaṃ māsaṃ haridre dve mūrāśaileya caṃdanaṃ ||

vacācaṃpakamustaṃ ca sarvauṣadhyo daśa smṛtāḥ |
ikṣavastṛṇarājaṃ ca niṣyā vā jāti dhānyakaṃ ||

vikāravacca gokṣīraṃ kusuṃbhaṃ kuṃkumaṃ tathā |
lavaṇaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ tatra saubhāgyāṣṭakamucyate ||

āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi dadhyakṣata tilāstathā |
yavāḥ siddhyarthakāśceti hyarghoṣṭāṃgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||

rajāṃsi paṃcavarṇāni maṃḍalārthaṃ hi kārayet |
śālitaṃḍūlacūrṇena śuklaṃ vāyavasaṃbhavaṃ ||

raktaṃ kusumasiṃdūraṃ gairikādi samudravaṃ |
haritālo dravaṃ pītaṃ rajanī saṃbhavaṃ tathā ||

dūrvāya * kuraścaiva vālakaṃ cūtapallavāḥ |
haridrā dvaya siddhārthaṃ śikhipatroraga tvacaḥ ||

kaṃkaṇauṣadhayaścaitā kautukākhyā nava smṛtāḥ |
gajāśvarathyā valmīka saṃgamād vraja go kulāt ||

p. 25b)

mṛdamānīya kuṃbheṣu prakṣipecca tvarā tathā |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmramārakūṭaṃ tathaiva ca ||

lauhaṃ * pu tathā sīsaṃdhātavaḥ saptakīrtitaṃ |
yamagodhūmadhānyāni tilākaṃgustathaiva ca ||

śyāmākaṃcanakaṃ caiva saptadhānyamudāhṛtaṃ |
vrīhayaśca yavāścaiva godhūmāḥ kaṃgukāstilāḥ ||

priyaṃgavaḥ kovidarāḥ koradūrṣāḥ satāṃnakāḥ |
māṣāmudgāsasūrāśca nivyāvāḥ sakulasthakaḥ ||

āḍhakyaścaṇakāścaiva gaṇaḥ saptadaśaḥ smṛtaḥ |
yavagodhūmadhānyāni tilāṃkaṃgukulatthakaḥ ||

māṣāmudmāmasūrāni niṣyāvāḥ śyāmasarṣapāḥ |
gavedhūkāśca nīvārā āḍhakyotha gatīnakā ||

caṇakāścīnakāścaiva dhānyāṣṭā daśaśaiva tu |
mūlapatrakarī rogra phalakāṃjadhirūḍhakāḥ ||

tvak puṣpaṃ kavakaṃ ceti śākaṃ daśavidhaṃ smṛtaṃ |
haimarājatatāmrāśca mṛnmayā lakṣaṇānvitā ||

yātrodvāha pratiṣṭhādo kuṃbhāsyurabhiṣecane |
paṃcāṇāṃgula vai putyā? utsedhe ṣoḍaśāṃgulāḥ ||

dvādaśāṃgulamūlā syurmukhamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ bhavet |
anukta dravya tatsaṃkhyā devatā pratimā priye ||

sauvarṇī rājatī tāmrī vṛkṣajāmārtikā tathā |
citta?jāpittalepotthā nijavṛttānurūpataḥ ||

āmāṣātpalaparyaṃtā kartavyāḥ śāḍhyavarjitaiḥ |
aguṣṭhaparvādārabhya vitastyavadhikā smṛtāḥ ||

aṃguṣṭhaparvādārabhya pādārabhyāvideva tu |
gṛhe tu pratimā kāryā nādhikāśasyate vudhaiḥ ||

p. 26a)

homograhādi pūjāyāṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ bhavet |
aṣṭāviṃśatiko cāpi yathāśakti vidhīyate ||

yathoktavastvasaṃpattau grāhyaṃ tadanukāriyat |
yavānāmiva godhūmā vrīhīṇāmiva śālayaḥ ||

ājyaṃ hutyamanādeśe juhotiṣu vidhīyate |
maṃtrasya devatāyāśca prajāpatiriti sthitiḥ ||

praṇavādi namoṃtaṃ ca caturthyāṃtaṃ ca sattama |
devatāyā svakaṃ nāma mūlamaṃtraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||

dadhyalābhe payaḥ kāryaṃ madhyalābhe tathā guḍaḥ |
ghṛtapratinidhiṃ kuryāt payo vā dadhi vā priye ||

ājyahomeṣu sarveṣu gavyameva bhave hṛtaṃ |
tadalābhe mahiṣyāstu ājamāvikameva tu ||

tadalābhe tu tailaṃ syāt tadalābhe tu jātilaṃ |
mahāgniṣo mayāvi ca daridraścaiva jāyate ||

tasmāt samiddhe hotavyaṃ nāsamiddhe kathaṃcana |
karṣamātramidaṃ home śuktimātraṃ payaḥ smṛtaṃ ||

uktāni paṃcagavyāni śuktimātrāṇi sādhubhiḥ |
tatsamaṃ madhudugdhetragrāsamātramudāhṛtaṃ ||

dadhiprasṛtimātraṃ syāt lājāsyurmuṣṭi saṃmitāḥ |
pṛthukāstat pramāṇāsyuḥ śaktavopi tathā vidhā ||

palārddhaṃ guḍamānaṃ ca śarkarāpi tathā vidhā |
grāsārddhaṃ mātraṃ matnānāmikṣuḥ parvapramāṇakaḥ ||

ekaṃ syāt patra puṣpaṃ ca tathā dhūpādi kalpayet |
mātu liṃgaṃ catuḥkhaṃḍaṃ panasaṃ daśadhā kṛtaṃ ||

aṣṭadhā nālikeraṃ ca caturdhā kadalīphalam |

p. 26b)

tridhā kṛtaṃ phalaṃ vailvaṃ kapitthaṃ khaṃḍitaṃ dvidhā ||

vrīhayo muṣṭimānāsyurmudgāmāṣāyavāstathā |
taṃḍulāsyustadardhāṃśo kodravāmuṣṭisaṃmitā ||

lavaṇaṃ śuktimātraṃ syānmarīcānyekaviṃśati |
ghṛtasya karṣiko homaḥ kṣīrasya madhurastathā ||

śuktimātrāhutirdadhnaḥ prasṛtiḥ pāyasasya tu |
khaṃḍatrayaṃ tu mūlānāṃ phalānāṃ svapramāṇataḥ ||

grāsamātraṃ juhotavyā itareṣāṃ ca taṃḍulāḥ |
akṣatāstu yavāḥ proktā abhāve vrīhayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||

tada bhāve tu godhūmā na tu khaṃḍatilājavaḥ |
yeṣāṃkaṣāṃ cidanyeṣāṃ dravyāṇāmatha saṃbhave ||

sarvatrā hyamupādeyaṃ bharadvāja munermatāt |
sarvapramāṇamākutyā sūkṣmāṇi paṃca paṃca ca ||

ikṣuṇāṃ parvakaṃ mānaṃ latānāmaṃguladvayaṃ |
caṃdraṃ vadana kāśmīra kastūrī yakṣakardamāt ||

kalāyaṃ saṃtitā netān gugguluṃ vadarāsthivat |
dravyaṃ sruveṇa hotavyaṃ pāṇinā kaṭhinaṃ haviḥ ||

sruva pūrṇāḥ dravāḥ proktāḥ kathināgrā samātrakāḥ |
vrīhayo yavagodhūma priyaṃgutilaśālayaḥ ||

svarūpeṇaiva hotavyā itareṣāṃrataṃḍulāḥ |
vālāgnihotriṇāṃ vipraṃ surūpaṃ ca guṇānvitaṃ ||

sapatnīkaṃ ca saṃpūjya bhūṣayitvā vibhūṣaṇaiḥ |
purohitaṃ mukhātamaṃ kṛtvānyāṃśca tathā dvijaḥ ||

caturviṃśadguṇopetān sapatnīkānnimaṃtritān |
ahatāṃ ca rasaṃ channān sragviṇaḥ śucibhūṣitān ||

p. 27a)

vastrayugmaṃ yathoṣṇīṣaṃ kuṃḍalaṃ kaṃṭhabhūṣaṇaṃ |
aṃgulībhūṣaṇaṃ caiva maṇivaṃdha sa bhūṣaṇaṃ ||

etāni caiva sarvāṇi prāraṃbhe dharmakarmaṇāṃ |
purohitā padatvātha ṛtvigbhyaḥ saṃpradāpayet ||

pūrvokta bhūṣaṇaṃ sarvaṃ soṣṇīṣaṃ vastrasaṃyutaṃ |
dadyāde nṛtprayoktasya ācchādana padaṃ tathā ||

saṃpūjya madhuparkeṇa ṛtvijaḥ karmakārayet |
apūjyāt kārayet karma kilviṣe naiva pūjyate ||

hemāsaṃkāriṇaḥ kāryāḥ paṃcaviṃśati ṛtvijaḥ |
dakṣayecca samaṃ sarvān nācārye dviguṇaṃ bhavet ||

prāgudīcyāṃ gatā rekhā kuryādekonaviṃśatiḥ |
khaṃḍeṃdu stripadaḥ koṇāśṛṃkhalā paṃcabhiḥ padaiḥ ||

caturviṃśat padā vāpīpalidhirviṃśatiḥ padaiḥ |
madhya ṣoḍaśabhiḥ koṣṭhaiḥ padmamaṣṭadalaṃ smṛtaṃ ||

śvetaṃ duḥśṛṃkhalā kṛṣṇāvallīnālena pūrayet |
bhadrāruṇāsitā vāpī paridhiḥ pītavarṇakaḥ ||

vāhyāṃtala dalaśvetā karṇikā pītakarṇikā |
paridhā veṣṭitaṃ padmaṃ vāhyaṃtarasarajastamaḥ ||

tanmadhye sthāpayed devāṃ brahmādyānstu surāsuran |
caturviṃśatirālekhyā rekhā prāgdakṣiṇāyataḥ ||

koṇeṣu śṛṃkhalā paṃca padāvalyastu pārśvataḥ |
padairnavabhirālekhyāścaturbhi laghuśṛṃkhalāḥ ||

laghuvṛttyapadaiḥ ṣaḍbhistatoṣṭāvaśabhiḥ padaiḥ |
kṛtvā liṃgāni vāpyaḥ syustrayodaśabhiraṃtarā ||

tato vāṃthī dvaya naiva pīṭhaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tasyā pādā paṃcapadā dvārāṇapi tathaiva ca ||

p. 27b)

ekāśīti padaṃ madhye padmaṃ svastika mucyate |
śṛṃkhalāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇena vallīnelena pūrayet ||

lakṣena śṛṃkhalā laghvī vallīḥ pītena pūrayet |
liṃgāni kṛṣṇavarṇāni śvete vāpi ca vāpikāḥ ||

pīṭhaṃ sa pārśve te naiva pīte na dvārapūraṇaṃ |
madhye syuḥ śṛṃkhalā raktāḥ vallīṃ nīlena pūrayet ||

bhadrāṇi pītaparvāṇi pītapaṃkaja karṇikā |
dalāni śvetavarṇāni yadvā citrāṇi kalpayet ||

tisro rekhā vahiḥ kāryā sitaraktāḥ sitāḥ kramāt |
rekhādaṣṭā daśa proktāścaturliṃga samudbhavet ||

koṇeṃdustripadaḥ śvetastripadaiḥ kṛṣṇa śṛṃkhalāḥ |
vallī sadya padā nīlā bhadraṃ raktaṃ catuṣpadaṃ ||

bhadrapārśvai mahārudre kṛṣṇamaṣṭādaśaiḥ padaiḥ |
śivasya pārśvato vāṇa dakṣiṇe raktavarṇakāḥ ||

padamekaṃ tathā pītaṃ bhadraṃ vāpyastu madhyataḥ |
śirasi śṛṃkhalāyāśca kuryāt pāne pada trayaṃ ||

liṃgānāṃ skaṃdhataḥ koṣṭhā viṃśatī rakta parṇakāḥ |
paridhiḥ pītavarṇestu padaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhismṛtaḥ ||

padaistu navabhipaścād raktaṃ padmaṃ sakarṇikaṃ |
prāgudīcyā yathā rekhāḥ ṣaḍviṃśaddhi prakalpayet ||

padāni dvādaśaśataṃ paṃcaviṃśatireva ca |
khaḍeṃdusti padaḥ koṇe śṛṃkhalā ṣaṭpadaiḥ smṛtāḥ ||

trayodaśa padāvallī bhadraṃ turayabhiḥ padaiḥ |
trayodaśa padā vāpī liṃgamaṣṭādaśaiḥ smṛtaṃ ||

liṃgatrayasya paṃktau tu śobhā koṣṭhāścaturdaśaḥ |

p. 28a)

teṣā su paripaktau tu koṣṭhāḥ saptadaśaiva tu ||

pūjāpaktistu vijñeyā paritaḥ parikalpitāḥ |
pūjāpaktyaṃtarāpaṃktau koṣṭhadvyaśīti saṃkhyayā ||

paridhi sa ca vijñeyo maṃḍalaraṃtarā dvayoḥ |
paridhvaṃtarakoṣṭheṣurarcato bhadramālikhet ||

viśeṣaścātra vijñeyaḥ śṛṃkhalā ṣaṭpadā bhavet |
trayodaśa padāvallī bhadraṃ tu navasaḥ padaiḥ ||

paṃcaviṃśatpadā vāpīritubhiḥ ṣoḍaśātmakaḥ |
madhyena vai padaṃ patre karṇikā keśarānvitaṃ ||

satvaṃ rajastamo varṇāḥ patito maṃḍalasya tu |
trayaḥ paridhayaḥ kāryāstatra dvārāṇi kārayet ||

siteṃduḥ śṛṃkhalā kṛṣṇā vallī nīlā prakīrtitā |
bhadraṃ vai vāruṇaṃ jñeyaṃ vāpī syācchvetavarṇikā ||

liṃgāni kṛṣṇavarṇāni pārśvato dvādaśaiva tu |
paridhiḥ pītavarṇaḥ syāt kamalaṃ paṃcavarṇakam ||

iti śrīvārāhītantre nirvāṇapade saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 7 ||


śrīdevyuvāca ||

devadeva jagannātha bhaktānugrahakāraṇaḥ |
sarvāpatya samāpnoti pavitrānaṃ kathaṃ naraḥ ||

īśvara uvāca ||

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi guhyāguhyantaraṃ paraṃ |
āpadudhāraṇaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutaṃ ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ caiva japtvā dhyātvā ca kālikāṃ |
iṃdrajidvandhanānmukta purādevi sacīpatiḥ ||

bhairavosya ṛṣiḥchando gāyatrī devatā prabhuḥ |
kālikā viniyogosya sarvāpadvinivāraṇe ||

oṃ śiro me kālikā pātu krīmādyāṃ ṅe yutānapiḥ |
lalāṭe caṃḍikā pātu kaṃṭhaṃ pātu parāṃvikā ||

p. 28b)

cāmuṃḍā hṛdayaṃ pātu nābhiṃmaṃgalacaṃḍikā |
ugracaṃḍā śiraḥ pātu pracaṃḍā pātu padayugaṃ ||

bhairavī tvaritā durgā cāmuṃḍā caṇḍanāsikā |
jayadurgā viśālākṣī bhadrakāli kapālini ||

śivadūtītitāpāṃtu daśadikṣu ca māṃ sadā |
tārākāli śivā svāhā jayaṃtī maṃgalā svadhā ||

yogini yoganiṃdā ca chinnamastā ca ḍākini |
rājadvāre jale'raranya saṃyoge saṃkaṭe grahe ||

rājñaḥ khaḍganipāte ca kārāgārādi bhīṣaṇe |
iti sarvatra * rastu sadā māṃ paramo yudhaiḥ ||

idaṃ ca kathitaṃ devi kavacaṃ brahmarūpinaṃ |
āpaduddhāraṇaṃ nāma sarvāpadvinivārakaṃ ||

asyā vidhāraṇād devi sarvāpannāsi yat kṣaṇā |
devyai puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā kavacaṃ prapaṭhet sakṛt ||

sarvāpannāśayet satyaṃ tatkṣaṇān nātra saṃśayaḥ |
trivāra paṭhanāt satyaṃ nāśayitvā'khilāpadaṃ ||

sarva priyatamo bhūtvā sarvaiśvaryamavāpnuyāt |
lohayaṃjalamadhyastho galapadvaṃdhanai dṛḍhaṃ ||

devīṃ hṛdi samārādhya paṭhitvāṣṭādhikaṃ śataṃ |
khaḍgādibhiḥravadhyasyādvaṃdhanānmucyate dhruvaṃ ||

guruvaktrāt samāsādye lakṣaṃ yaḥ prapaṭhecchuciḥ |
parituṣṭā tadā kālī tasya pratyakṣatāṃ vrajet ||

devyuvāca ||

devadeva mahādeva sarvāgama viśāradā |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi mudrāścaiva tu lakṣaṇaṃ ||

p. 29a)

bhairava uvāca ||

ṣṛṇu devi pravakṣyami mudrāyāścaiva lakṣaṇaṃ |
saṃmukhī kṛtya hasau dvau dvau hastau kiṃ citāṃgulī ||

mukulī tu samākhyātā paṃkajaṃ prasṛtaiva sā |
pūrvoktā mukulīyā ca pradeśe niḥsṛtāṃguliḥ ||

vyokeśa mudrā mukulā padmamudrāṃ pradarśayet |
aṃguṣṭhaiva citījau tu svakīyāṃguli veṣṭitau ||

uccaicābhimukhau hastau yojayitvā tu niṣṭurā |
tarjanyo kucite kṛtvā tathaiva ca kanīṣṭhayo ||

adhomukhīdaṣṭa nakhā sthitā madhye karasya tu |
catapraścotthitāḥ pṛṣṭhe aṃguṣṭhā vekataḥ kuru ||

nālaṃ vyavasthitau dvau tu vyomamudrā prakīrtitā |
devatā tena saṃtuṣṭā sarvadā sanmukhī bhavet ||

aṃguṣṭhau nikṣipet seyaṃ mudrātvā vāhanī matā |
saṃgrathyanikṣipet seyaṃ mudrā vā vāhanī smṛtā ||

adhomukhī tviyaṃ cet syāt sthāpanī mudrikā smṛtā |
ucchritā vucchitau kuryāt saṃmukhī karaṇī bhavet ||

prasṛtāṃgulikau hastau mithaḥ śliṣṭau tu sanmukhaiḥ |
kuyāt svahṛdayaṃ soyaṃ mudrā prārthanasaṃjñikā ||

ityeva sarvadevānāṃ pūjane ṣaṭpradarśayet |
ucchritaṃ dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭhaṃ vāmāṃguṣṭhena vaṃdhayet ||

vāmāṃgulārdakṣinābhiraṃgulībhiśca veṣṭayet |
liṃgamudreya mākhyātā śiva sānnidhya kāriṇī ||

śrīkāmaḥ śīrṣṇi kurvīta rājyakāmastu netrayoḥ |
mukhe tvatradya kāmastu grīvāyāṃ rogaśāṃti kṛt ||

p. 29b)

hṛdaya sarvakāmī ca jñānārthī nābhimaṃḍale |
rājyakāmastu guhyai vai rāṣṭrakāmastu pādayoḥ ||

āvāhanī sthāpanī ca saṃnidhī karaṇī tathā |
susaṃnirodhanī mudrā saṃmukhī karaṇī tathā ||

saṃkalī karaṇā caiva mahāmudrā tathaiva ca |
śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadhanukaustubhagāruḍāḥ ||

śrīvatsava na māle ca yonimudrāṃ pradarśayet |
etābhiḥ saptadaśabhirmudrābhistu vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

āvāhanīyaṃ mudrā syād devārcana vidhau priye |
eṣaivādho mukhī mudrā sthāpane śasyate punaḥ ||

unnatāṃguṣṭhayogena muṣṭhī hṛdayakā dvayaṃ |
sannidhīkaraṇī mudrāddevārcana vidhau priye ||

aṃguṣṭhagarbhiṇī caiva mudrā syāt saṃnirodhinī |
uttānamuṣṭi yugalā saṃmukhī karaṇī matā ||

aṃgairevāṃga vinyāsaḥ sakalā karaṇī bhavet |
anyonyāṃguṣṭha sa lagnā vistārita kara dvayī ||

mahāmudreyamākhyātā nyunādhika samāpanī |
kaniṣṭhānāmikā madhyāṃ tasyāṃguṣṭha tadagrataḥ ||

gopitāṃguṣṭhamūlena saṃraddhā mukulī kṛtā |
karadvayena mudrā syācchaṃkhākhyeyaṃ surārcane ||

anyonyābhimukhasparśaṃ vyatyayaṃ na tu veṣṭayet |
aṃgulibhiḥ prayatnena maṃḍalīkaraṇaṃ priye ||

cakramudreyamākhyātā gadāmudrā tataḥ paraṃ |
anyonyābhimukhā śliṣṭā tataḥ kaustubha saṃjñakā ||

kaniṣṭhe nyonya saṃlagnebhimukhe hi parasparaṃ |
vāmasya tarjanī madhye madhyānāmikayorapi ||

p. 30a)

vāmāṃnāmikasaṃsṛṣṭā tarjanī madhya śobhitā |
paryāyeṇānatīguṣṭha dvayī kaustubha lakṣaṇā ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikā madhya tarjanyagra karadvayī |
mune śrīvatsa mudreyaṃ vanamālā bhavet tataḥ ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikā madhyāmuṣṭhirunnatatarjanī |
paribhrāṃtāṇirasyuccestarjanībhyāṃ divaukasaḥ ||

yonimudrā samākhyātā dyotatkāra dvayāśritā |
tarjanyā kṛṣṭhamadhyāṃte sthitānāmika yugmikā ||

madhyasthalasthitāṃguṣṭhe yaṃ śastāmarcana iti |
aṣṭatriṃśat ṣoḍaśa vā daśapaṃcopacārakā ||

tāni bhajya pravakṣyāmi ketetaiśca kṛte ca kiṃ |
arghyaṃ pādyādyā camane madhuparkamupaspṛśaṃ ||

snānaṃ nīrāṃjanaṃ vastramācamyacopavītakaṃ |
punarācamane bhūṣadarpaṇālokanaṃ tataḥ ||

gaṃdhapuṣpe dhūpadīpo naivedyaṃ ca tataḥ kramāt |
pānīyaṃtoyamācāraṃ hastavāsastataḥ paraṃ ||

tāṃvūlamanulepaṃ ca puṣpadānaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
gītavādyaṃ tathā nṛtyaṃ stutiṃ caiva pradakṣiṇaṃ ||

puṣpāṃjali namaskārā cāṣṭā triṃśat samīritā |
āsanaṃ svāgataṃ cārghyaṃ pādyamācamanīyakaṃ ||

madhuparkāsanasnā ca vasanābharaṇāni ca |
sugaṃdhaḥ sumano dhūpo dīpa sannena tarpaṇaṃ ||

mālānulepane naiva namaskāra visarjane |
arghyapādyācamānyava madhuparkā ca mānyapi ||

gaṃdhādayoni nidrāṃnā upacārādaśakramā |

p. 30b)

gaṃdhapuṣpairdhūpadīpau naivedyaṃ paṃcamasmṛtaḥ ||

gaṃdhatailamatho dadyād devasya pratimāṃ tataḥ |
pūrvā ca viṣṇukrāṃtā ca śyāmākaṃ padmameva ca ||

pādyāṃgāni ca catvāri kathitāni samāsataḥ |
karpūramagaraṃ puṣpa dravyāṇyācamanīyake ||

siddhārthamakṣataṃ caiva dūrvā tilakameva ca |
paṃcagaṃdhaṃ phalaṃ puṣpamaṣṭāṃgaṃ tvarghyamucyate ||

snāne vastre tathā bhakṣye dadyādācamanīyakaṃ |
rajanī saha devī ca śirīṣaṃ lakṣmaṇāpi ca ||

mahābhadrā kuśāgrāṇi udvartanamihocyate |
akṣatāgaṃdhapuṣpāṇi snāna pātre tathā trayaṃ ||

dravyāmeva pradātavyāḥ kṣālitāstaṃḍulāḥ śubhāḥ |
tathā ca mana pātrepi dadyājjātī phalaṃ priye ||

lavaṃgamapi kaṃkolaṃ śastamācamanīyakaṃ |
taṃḍūlāt prakṣipet teṣu dravyābhāvenu tat smaran ||

pratimāpaṭa yaṃtrāṇāṃ nitya snānaṃ na kārayet |
kārayet sarva divase yadā vā maladhāraṇaṃ ||

nākṣatairarcayed viṣṇuṃ na tulasyāgaṇādhipaṃ |
na dūrvayā jayed devīṃ vilvapatrairdivākaraṃ ||

mahābhiṣekaṃ sarvatra śaṃkha naiva prakalpayet |
sarvatraiva praśastoktaḥ śivasūryārcanaṃ vinā ||

maṃdāraṃ pārijātaṃ ca vaśya saubhāgya dāyakaṃ |
pītajāta sarvakālaṃ viśeṣastaṃbhanehitaṃ ||

kharjūrapuṣpaṃ jātī ca kuṃda śrīvṛddhidāyakaṃ |
vaṃdhūkaṃ mokṣasaubhāgyaṃ japā ca tripurā priye ||

utpalaṃ dhanavṛddhiṃ ca padmaṃ pauṣṭikameva ca |
punnāga sarvasaṃpattivabhrūkaṃ dhanahānikaṃ ||

p. 31a)

kahlādohlādasiddhiṃ ca ketakīṃ ca vivarjayet |
muhuśca nisphalaṃ jñeyaṃ mallikā duḥkhadāyinā ||

campakai putra kāmastu trisaṃdhyā duḥkhamocane |
droṇaṃ mokṣamavāpnoti sevaṃtī sarvasiddhidā ||

sephālikājayakarī karṇikā vṛddhidāyikā |
kumude prītidaṃ caiva sudarśane dhanapradaṃ ||

kuruṃṭha siddhi saubhāgyaṃ nīladravyaṃ tu hārikāṃ |
damana sarvasaṃpatti bilvapatraṃ parāpriyaṃ ||

nirgaṃdhotkaṭagaṃdhacca varjayeccakra pūjane |
kṛṣṇāparājitā mṛtyu sutamālī tathaiva ca ||

mallikānāma saṃjñāyā duḥkhadā mṛtyudā sadā |
ṛdivyādhi pradaścaiva keśayuktaśca hānikaṃ ||

krimimaya mahābhīti rājadaṃda mahadbhayaṃ |
javā puṣpamaheśāni pūrvavat pūjayecchivāṃ ||

māsamātreṇa haṃtyeva pātakaṃ śatajanmakaṃ |
saubhāgyaṃ vāgbhave maṃtrī tripurāyā prasādataḥ ||

śvetapuṣpairmaheśāni mahadbhiḥ pūjayannaraḥ |
nāśayet pāṭakaṃ sarvamāsamātraṃ ca pūjayet ||

trilokaṃ vaśagaṃ tasya sarvapāpaṃ dahed budhaḥ |
bilvapatreśca jalajaiḥ satyaśca paripūjayet ||

pūrvavat parameśāni māsamātraṃ prasannadhīḥ |
samṛddhivān bhaven maṃtrī sarvapāpaṃ haret sadā ||

mallimālati jātī ca kuṃdaiśca śatapatrakaiḥ |
